# Better Call Saul：Chunks 积累

## S01E01

### 1. bully sb. into doing sth.

**意思：** 用威逼迫使某人做某事

> Don't let false allegations bully you into an unfair fight.

**来源：** S01E01 · 68，00:05:14,735–00:05:18,704

### 2. have sb. cornered

**意思：** 使某人陷入困境／无路可退

> When legal forces have you cornered, better call Saul!

**来源：** S01E01 · 71，00:05:24,223–00:05:27,591

### 3. Which brings us to…

**意思：** 这就引出了……

> Which brings us to these three...

**来源：** S01E01 · 110，00:09:03,202–00:09:05,369

### 4. That's a bit of a reach.

**意思：** 这有点牵强／推得太远了

> That's a bit of a reach... Don't you think, Dave?

**来源：** S01E01 · 123，00:09:42,992–00:09:45,576

### 5. Call me crazy, but…

**意思：** 你可以说我想法离谱，但……

> Call me crazy, but I don't think they deserve to have their bright futures ruined by a momentary, minute, never-to-be-repeated lapse of judgment.

**来源：** S01E01 · 129–132，00:10:01,677–00:10:12,436

### 6. a lapse of judgment

**意思：** 一时判断失误

> never-to-be-repeated lapse of judgment.

**来源：** S01E01 · 132，00:10:08,651–00:10:12,436

### 7. You're bigger than that.

**意思：** 你有更大的气量／不至于计较到那种地步

> Ladies and gentlemen, you're bigger than that.

**来源：** S01E01 · 133，00:10:12,488–00:10:17,524

### 8. Since when does sth. matter?

**意思：** 什么时候开始这也算事了？／怎么现在反倒在意这个？质疑某标准，常带不满

> So, since when does that matter?!

**来源：** S01E01 · 171，00:12:24,954–00:12:27,237

### 9. It'll be a cold day in hell before…

**意思：** 我绝不会……／除非太阳打西边出来（强烈、粗俗）

> 'Cause it'll be a cold day in hell before I do any more P.D. work for this shitty court!

**来源：** S01E01 · 176–177，00:12:35,998–00:12:39,967

### 10. I'm fuzzy as to why/how …

**意思：** 我不太明白……

> Well, I'm just fuzzy as to why you think he needs a lawyer.

**来源：** S01E01 · 214，00:15:18,661–00:15:23,630

### 11. be beyond reproach

**意思：** 无可指责，挑不出毛病

> I mean, Craig, the way you run your office is beyond reproach.

**来源：** S01E01 · 215，00:15:23,666–00:15:27,834

### 12. sth. may be in order

**意思：** 也许该做一点……／……可能是合适的

> I just think a little proactivity may be in order.

**来源：** S01E01 · 236，00:16:11,880–00:16:15,098

### 13. clear sth. up

**意思：** 把事情弄清楚／消除误会

> "Talk to me," he says. Uh, "help me clear this thing up.

**来源：** S01E01 · 245，00:16:34,703–00:16:37,654

### 14. go south

**意思：** 开始出问题／变糟

> Hey, that's when it all goes south.

**来源：** S01E01 · 248，00:16:40,909–00:16:43,493

### 15. have sb. in your corner

**意思：** 有人站在你这边、支持你

> That's when you want someone in your corner,

**来源：** S01E01 · 249，00:16:43,545–00:16:45,495

### 16. fight tooth and nail

**意思：** 竭尽全力争取／拼命抗争

> someone who will fight tooth and nail.

**来源：** S01E01 · 250，00:16:45,547–00:16:48,999

### 17. sleep on it

**意思：** 先不急着决定，考虑一晚再说

> I think maybe we should sleep on it.

**来源：** S01E01 · 262，00:17:34,194–00:17:37,578

### 18. come out of nowhere

**意思：** 突然冒出来／出乎意料地出现

> I was making a turn! He came out of nowhere!

**来源：** S01E01 · 298，00:19:26,285–00:19:27,951

### 19. make things right / make it right

**意思：** 把问题补救好／作出弥补

> What are you gonna do to make things right? I don't know, fellas. What can I do to make it right?

**来源：** S01E01 · 317–319，00:19:58,886–00:20:14,252

### 20. make a check out to sb.

**意思：** 把支票收款人写成某人

> And why was it made out to me... Why not Chuck?

**来源：** S01E01 · 375，00:25:35,382–00:25:38,383

### 21. I just assumed (that) + 从句

**意思：** 我只是默认／未经确认就以为……

> I just assumed it was best to have the money go through you.

**来源：** S01E01 · 377–378，00:25:43,085–00:25:46,302

### 22. have the money go through sb.

**意思：** 安排钱经过某人这个环节，由其接收／转交处理

> I just assumed it was best to have the money go through you.

**来源：** S01E01 · 377–378，00:25:43,085–00:25:46,302

### 23. This isn't going away.

**意思：** 这件事不会就这么算了／不会自行消失

> This isn't going away, Howard.

**来源：** S01E01 · 380，00:25:48,757–00:25:51,141

### 24. in that ballpark

**意思：** 大约在那个数额／范围

> Plus $17 million... In that ballpark.

**来源：** S01E01 · 391，00:26:18,086–00:26:21,871

### 25. do right by sb.

**意思：** 公平对待某人／对得起某人

> It's time to do right by him and cash him out.

**来源：** S01E01 · 397，00:26:32,968–00:26:35,969

### 26. do what's in sb.'s best interests

**意思：** 做对某人最有利的事

> I'm just doing what's in his - best interests. - So am I.

**来源：** S01E01 · 404–405，00:26:52,737–00:26:56,289

### 27. get caught up in sth.

**意思：** 过度沉浸／陷在某种想法或活动中，以至于忽略其他事

> You know, Jimmy, sometimes, in our line of work, you can get so... Caught up in the idea of winning that you forget to listen to your heart.

**来源：** S01E01 · 426–428，00:27:59,554–00:28:10,029

### 28. I figured…

**意思：** 我就想……／我判断……

> Yeah, I knew you missed it, so I figured, "what the heck?"

**来源：** S01E01 · 453–454，00:33:01–00:33:04

### 29. what the heck?

**意思：** 此处是“管它呢，买吧”，放下顾虑后决定做某事

> Yeah, I knew you missed it, so I figured, "what the heck?"

**来源：** S01E01 · 453–454，00:33:01–00:33:04

### 30. I don't expect you to do sth.

**意思：** 此处：我并不要求你……；也可表示不认为你会……

> I don't expect you to go out of pocket.

**来源：** S01E01 · 458，00:33:10–00:33:12

### 31. go out of pocket

**意思：** 自掏腰包承担费用

> I don't expect you to go out of pocket.

**来源：** S01E01 · 458，00:33:10–00:33:12

### 32. Can you not do sth. for a second?

**意思：** 你能不能先别……，暂停一下？（可能带不耐烦）

> Hey, Chuck, um, can you not read that for a second?

**来源：** S01E01 · 461，00:33:19–00:33:22

### 33. You've got to do sth.

**意思：** 你必须……／你非得……不可

> You got to cash out.（467，00:33:36–00:33:41）；I know that you don't want to hear it, but you've got to.（470，00:33:46–00:33:49）

### 34. cash out

**意思：** 将持有的资产／权益变现，拿钱退出

> You got to cash out.

**来源：** S01E01 · 467，00:33:36–00:33:41

### 35. There's no other way.

**意思：** 没有其他办法了，只能这样

> Again? Really? There's no other way.

**来源：** S01E01 · 469，00:33:43–00:33:46；本条字幕未标明说话人切换

### 36. I know (that) you don't want to hear it, but…

**意思：** 我知道你不想听这话，但……

> I know that you don't want to hear it, but you've got to.

**来源：** S01E01 · 470，00:33:46–00:33:49

### 37. beat this

**意思：** 战胜眼前的困难

> You know I'm going to beat this.

**来源：** S01E01 · 471，00:33:49–00:33:52

### 38. take sth. to its logical conclusion

**意思：** 顺着某个想法／决定的逻辑推演到底，看看最终后果

> Let's take this to its logical conclusion.

**来源：** S01E01 · 484，00:34:17–00:34:20

### 39. pay out sb.'s share

**意思：** 支付某人所占份额对应的钱

> In order to pay out my share, suppose my partners are forced to liquidate the firm.

**来源：** S01E01 · 485–486，00:34:22–00:34:26

### 40. suppose 从句…

**意思：** 假设……

> In order to pay out my share, suppose my partners are forced to liquidate the firm.

**来源：** S01E01 · 485–486，00:34:22–00:34:26

### 41. Then what?

**意思：** 然后呢？／那接下来怎么办？追问下一步或后果

> Then what?

**来源：** S01E01 · 487，00:34:26–00:34:28

### 42. be out in the cold

**意思：** 被晾在一边，得不到支持

> My clients are out in the cold.

**来源：** S01E01 · 488，00:34:28–00:34:30

### 43. be scattered to the winds

**意思：** 四散，七零八落

> My cases are scattered to the winds.

**来源：** S01E01 · 489，00:34:30–00:34:32

### 44. What happens to sb./sth.?

**意思：** 那……会怎么样／怎么办？追问受影响的人或事

> What happens to your cronies in the mailroom, the assistants, the paralegals, the janitorial staff?

**来源：** S01E01 · 491–492，00:34:35–00:34:40

### 45. be out on the street

**意思：** 此处：失业、失去生计；也可指失去住处

> All of them, out on the street.

**来源：** S01E01 · 493，00:34:40–00:34:42

### 46. be over and done with

**意思：** 彻底结束、了结

> Your friend Kim... a promising career... over and done with.

**来源：** S01E01 · 494，00:34:42–00:34:44

### 47. All the more reason to/not to do sth.

**意思：** 那就更有理由……／更不应该……

> Let's not exaggerate. I helped. You helped. All the more reason not to tear it down just for a little bit of cash.

**来源：** S01E01 · 498–500，00:34:50,856–00:34:58,078

### 48. go under

**意思：** 撑不下去／财务上垮掉

> Look... Chuck, I'm going under, okay?

**来源：** S01E01 · 501，00:34:58,114–00:35:01,699

### 49. the whole nine yards

**意思：** 全套，一整套该做的都做了

> The whole nine yards.

**来源：** S01E01 · 508，00:35:18,467–00:35:19,850

### 50. have nowhere else to turn

**意思：** 没有其他地方／人可以求助

> You're representing people who have nowhere else to turn.

**来源：** S01E01 · 511，00:35:25,524–00:35:27,808

### 51. sth. is beside the point

**意思：** ……不是重点／与眼下要讨论的核心无关

> The money is beside the point. Money is not beside the point... Money is the point.

**来源：** S01E01 · 512–513，00:35:27,860–00:35:32,646

### 52. have sb. all worked up

**意思：** 让某人非常激动、焦虑或生气

> This is what has you all worked up?

**来源：** S01E01 · 531，00:36:16,108–00:36:18,075

### 53. make ends meet

**意思：** 勉强维持收支、支付生活所需

> And the two of you agreed that since, as everybody knows, you're going back to work any day now, that the firm should help you make ends meet.

**来源：** S01E01 · 545–547，00:36:48,557–00:36:55,479

### 54. pick up where sb. left off

**意思：** 从先前停下的地方继续

> I'm gonna go back to work, and I'm picking up where I left off!

**来源：** S01E01 · 551–552，00:37:01,904–00:37:06,907

### 55. I understand (that) …, but…

**意思：** 我理解……，但……

> I understand that you're trying to look out for me, but you're missing the bigger picture.

**来源：** S01E01 · 555–556，00:37:38,991–00:37:45,079

### 56. look out for sb.

**意思：** 照顾／维护某人的利益

> I understand that you're trying to look out for me,

**来源：** S01E01 · 555，00:37:38,991–00:37:42,576

### 57. miss the bigger picture

**意思：** 忽略整体情况／大局

> but you're missing the bigger picture.

**来源：** S01E01 · 556，00:37:42,611–00:37:45,079

### 58. It's simply a matter of sth.

**意思：** 这只是……的问题／无非是……

> It's simply a matter of professional courtesy.

**来源：** S01E01 · 573，00:38:30,709–00:38:34,661

### 59. ride on sb. else's coattails

**意思：** 借别人的成就、名声或地位获益

> Why ride on someone else's coattails?

**来源：** S01E01 · 578，00:38:51,931–00:38:55,182

### 60. have sth. dialed in

**意思：** 把某件事摸透、调到顺手状态

> Slippin' Jimmy had it dialed in, all right?

**来源：** S01E01 · 635，00:42:09,811–00:42:12,027

### 61. How's sth. sound?

**意思：** ……怎么样？（完整说法：How does sth. sound?）

> How's 2 grand sound?

**来源：** S01E01 · 654，00:42:56,691–00:42:58,757

### 62. play it cool

**意思：** 保持镇定／表现得若无其事

> I'm gonna play it cool,

**来源：** S01E01 · 706，00:44:54,308–00:44:55,608

### 63. I wouldn't think of doing sth.

**意思：** 我绝不会考虑做……

> I wouldn't think of, uh, taking your money for this.

**来源：** S01E01 · 727，00:45:46,814–00:45:49,348

### 64. talk sth. over

**意思：** 把某件事仔细商量一下

> Mm, yes, I'd... I'd be happy to talk it over.

**来源：** S01E01 · 729，00:45:51,819–00:45:55,187

### 65. run into sb.

**意思：** 偶然遇见某人

> Funny to run into you, Betsy.

**来源：** S01E01 · 745，00:47:38,506–00:47:39,972


## S01E02

### 1. hang in there

**意思：** 撑住，坚持一下（安慰／鼓励）

> Just hang in there. Hang in there.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:01:25–00:01:26

### 2. It's no joke.

**意思：** 这可不是闹着玩的／事情很严重

> Hit-and-run? It's no joke.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:02:11–00:02:13

### 3. It's up to sb.

**意思：** 由某人决定；此处也带“后果看你怎么做”的施压意味

> - Y... yeah, yeah, maybe. - That's up to you, man.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:02:19–00:02:22

### 4. play it smart

**意思：** 聪明点，作出明智选择（可带警告）

> Yeah, that's right... play it smart.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:02:43–00:02:45

### 5. by any chance

**意思：** 碰巧／有没有可能（缓和询问）

> Is there, by any chance, a Betsy Kettleman here?

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:06:47–00:06:51

### 6. look sb. in the eye

**意思：** 直视某人的眼睛

> See, I'm not sure if this is a situation where I should or should not look you in the eye.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:06:51–00:06:55

### 7. I'm not sure if…

**意思：** 我不确定是否……；委婉表达不确定

> See, I'm not sure if this is a situation where I should or should not look you in the eye.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:06:51–00:06:55

### 8. make an educated guess

**意思：** 根据已有信息作出有依据的推测

> I'm gonna make an educated guess what happened here.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:08:13–00:08:18

### 9. cross a line

**意思：** 越界，做了不该做的事

> Now, at this juncture, I'm deducing that they said or did something that... Crossed a line,

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:08:39–00:08:44

### 10. put sb. in their place

**意思：** 让某人认清分寸／教训其别太放肆（强势）

> and you, with some justification... You put them in their place.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:08:44–00:08:49

### 11. The bottom line…

**意思：** 说到底／最关键的是……（原句有停顿）

> The bottom line... not to be morbid,

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:08:55–00:08:58

### 12. jump to the nuclear option

**意思：** 直接动用最极端的手段（比喻，力度强）

> Why jump to the nuclear option?

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:09:18–00:09:20

### 13. lay eyes on sb./sth.

**意思：** 看见某人／某物；常用 never lay eyes on… 表示从未见过

> Neither you nor your lovely abuelita will ever lay eyes on us ever again, guaranteed...

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:09:27–00:09:31

### 14. Assuming…

**意思：** 假设……／前提是……

> Assuming, you know, that they're still breathing.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:09:37–00:09:41

### 15. You got a mouth on you.

**意思：** 你这张嘴可真厉害／你可真能说（此处指能言善辩；其他语境也可责备顶嘴或说话放肆）

> You got a mouth on you.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:09:51–00:09:54

### 16. cut sb. loose

**意思：** 放某人走／解除与某人的牵连；此处是放人

> So, what... I should cut 'em loose?

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:10:27–00:10:32

### 17. run a scam

**意思：** 实施骗局，行骗

> But I was running a scam.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:12:19–00:12:20

### 18. get my name out

**意思：** 让更多人知道我，打出名气

> This is a good case for me... A lot of publicity. I'll get my name out, and...

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:12:37–00:12:42

### 19. The joke's on sb.

**意思：** 结果某人反而成了笑柄／吃亏的人

> So, joke's on me. Ha ha!

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:13:06–00:13:07

### 20. That's all this is.

**意思：** 事情就是这么回事，仅此而已（限定问题的性质）

> I made a mistake. That's all this is.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:13:31–00:13:33

### 21. a known quantity

**意思：** 背景或表现为人所知、可以判断的人／事；此处强调身份可核实

> I'm a known quantity... I am!

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:14:34–00:14:35

### 22. the tip of the spear

**意思：** 行动的先锋／最前线（比喻，较郑重）

> I'm the tip of the spear, and releasing me would be the smart move.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:14:46–00:14:50

### 23. take full responsibility

**意思：** 承担全部责任（原文额外用 complete 强调）

> I take full and complete responsibility,

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:16:24–00:16:27

### 24. You really see sb. doing sth.?

**意思：** 你真觉得某人会做……？（常带怀疑，不是实际看见）

> You really see the FBI hiring those two flaquitos?

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:17:12–00:17:15

### 25. put sb. up to sth.

**意思：** 怂恿／指使某人做某事（常用于不当行为）

> They don't deserve to die. I put them up to it.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:19:11–00:19:14

### 26. the apple of sb.'s eye

**意思：** 某人最疼爱的人；原文临时用了复数 apples，不照搬为标准形式

> What... for herself? No. No. For them... for these two, her boys, her apples of her eye.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:19:48–00:19:55

### 27. That's on sb.

**意思：** 那是某人的责任／后果由某人承担（不是物理位置）

> No, that's on them.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:20:07–00:20:08

### 28. The way I see it,…

**意思：** 在我看来……（原文省略了 The）

> Way I see it, that's because you're tough, but you're fair.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:20:13–00:20:16

### 29. make the punishment fit the crime

**意思：** 让惩罚与过错相称；法律／道德讨论

> Wait. See, I'm advising... That you make the punishment fit the crime.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:21:06–00:21:12

### 30. head in the wrong direction

**意思：** 朝错误的方向发展；此处指谈判越谈越偏

> I think we're heading in the wrong direction.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:22:12–00:22:14

### 31. I ain't got all day.

**意思：** 我没时间一直等（不耐烦；标准表达 I haven't got all day）

> All right, lay 'em out! I ain't got all day!

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:23:07–00:23:10

### 32. Save your breath.

**意思：** 省省口舌吧／别白费力气说了（可显得冷淡）

> - You... you... you... aah! Aah! - Save your breath.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:24:30–00:24:32

### 33. talk sb. down from sth. to sth.

**意思：** 通过劝说把结果从严重程度降到较轻程度；此处夸张地比较死刑与缓刑

> Hey, I just talked you down from a death sentence... - Oh, my God! Oh, my! - ...to six months' probation.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:24:34–00:24:39

### 34. You didn't…, did you?

**意思：** 你没……吧？（求证，可能带担忧）

> Jimmy, you didn't drive like this, did you?

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:27:50–00:27:54

### 35. I may have done sth.

**意思：** 我可能做过……（保留对过去行为的不确定性）

> Emergency-room bill... Yeah, I may have glanced at it.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:31:47–00:31:51

### 36. I know how this looks.

**意思：** 我知道这看起来像什么／会让你怎么想（解释可疑情况）

> Hey, Chuck, listen, I... I know how this looks.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:31:55–00:31:59

### 37. be down to my last dime

**意思：** 只剩最后一点钱，几乎一文不剩

> I'm down to my last dime, and, suddenly, I'm paying for broken legs, but...

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:31:59–00:32:04

### 38. be a train wreck

**意思：** 糟糕得一团乱／处于严重失序状态（负面评价）

> - Petty with a prior. - Look, the parents are a train wreck.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:35:30–00:35:32

### 39. be light on sth.

**意思：** 某种东西不够／缺少；此处是停车券不足

> - You're light on stickers. - Come on.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:35:45–00:35:47

### 40. This is not a me problem. This is a you problem.

**意思：** 这不是我的问题，是你需要解决的问题（直接、推卸或划清责任）

> This is not a me problem... This is a you problem.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:36:59–00:37:03

### 41. meet sb. halfway

**意思：** 各让一步／作出折中，不是路上迎接

> - Petty with a prior. - Come on. Meet me halfway.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:37:33–00:37:35

### 42. freak sb. out

**意思：** 把某人吓坏／弄得惊慌不安

> Tuco freaked you out, huh?

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:42:00–00:42:04

### 43. north of + 数字

**意思：** 超过某个数字（金额等）

> Um, north of a million and a half bucks... I think.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:42:37–00:42:42

### 44. rip sb. off

**意思：** 骗取／抢走某人的钱财；此处计划盗取对方的钱

> - I'm gonna rip them off. - Oh. I like ripping off thieves 'cause they can't go to the cops... they have no recourse.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:42:55–00:43:04

### 45. I'm…, not…

**意思：** 我是……，不是……（强调身份、立场或界限）

> Hey, look, I'm a lawyer, not a criminal.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:43:44–00:43:48

### 46. I'm not doing that again, not ever.

**意思：** 我再也不会这样做了，绝不（强调决心）

> I'm not doing that again, not ever.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:43:57–00:43:59

### 47. God forbid

**意思：** 但愿不要发生（插入语，表示不希望某种坏事发生）

> A-and if you're ever in trouble, God forbid, legal trouble, I will be there, 24-7, as a lawyer.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:44:10–00:44:18

### 48. What's your angle?

**意思：** 你到底有什么目的／打的什么算盘？（怀疑隐藏动机）

> What's your angle?

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:44:18–00:44:21

### 49. As far as I'm concerned,…

**意思：** 在我看来／就我的立场而言……；不等于据我所知

> As far as I'm concerned, you're a client.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:44:28–00:44:31

### 50. be in the game

**意思：** 参与这套活动／卷入其中；此处指犯罪圈，不等于所有场景都指犯罪

> For when you figure out you're in the game. I'm not in the game. I... I promise.

**来源：** S01E02 · 00:45:04–00:45:11


## S01E03

### 1. a sight for sore eyes

**意思：** 见到就让人高兴的人／事物（欢迎久别之人）

> All right. You're a sight for sore eyes.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:00:57–00:00:59

### 2. come all this way

**意思：** 大老远赶来／特地跑这么远

> Sorry you had to come all this way.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:01:02–00:01:03

### 3. be in a bit of a pickle

**意思：** 遇上麻烦，陷入困境（口语，略带轻描淡写）

> Anyway. As you can see, I'm in a... A bit of a pickle.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:01:18–00:01:24

### 4. work your magic

**意思：** 施展你的本事，把事情搞定（可恭维或请求帮助）

> Let's talk strategy, okay? 'Cause I need you to work your magic and make this whole situation go poof.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:01:53–00:02:01

### 5. a plan of attack

**意思：** 行动方案／应对策略，不一定指军事攻击

> You got a plan of attack? Huh? Come on.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:02:01–00:02:04

### 6. I know I haven't made it easy for you, but…

**意思：** 我知道我让你为难／不好办了，但……（承认负担再请求）

> I know I haven't made it easy for you, but you are... you are the man!

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:02:04–00:02:10

### 7. a bag of tricks

**意思：** 一套拿手办法／各种招数（可褒可贬）

> Right? I know you got a million legal loopholes that we can dance through... Big bag of tricks, you know?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:02:10–00:02:18

### 8. be in it up to your neck

**意思：** 深陷某个麻烦／脱不开身；it 回指当前困境

> Now that you're in it up to your neck, you can't even call me yourself... you have mom call me?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:02:32–00:02:37

### 9. take it upon yourself to do sth.

**意思：** 自行决定／主动承担做某事（也可批评擅自行动）

> Mom took it upon herself to call you!

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:02:37–00:02:39

### 10. hear what sb. wants to hear

**意思：** 只听进自己想听的话，选择性理解

> She hears what she wants to hear, okay?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:02:48–00:02:50

### 11. It's about time (that) sb. did sth.

**意思：** 早该……了／是时候……了（did 不表示这件事已发生）

> And it's about time that I started to make both of us proud.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:03:22–00:03:28

### 12. So long as…

**意思：** 只要……／前提是……（此处引出帮助的条件）

> So long as you understand that.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:03:40–00:03:43

### 13. make a fool out of sb.

**意思：** 让某人出丑／显得像傻瓜

> Jimmy, if I do this... If... Do not make a fool out of me.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:03:45–00:03:53

### 14. Whatever it is, I'll do it.

**意思：** 不管是什么，我都愿意做（无条件承诺）

> Just tell me what to do. Whatever it is, I'll do it.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:04:01–00:04:09

### 15. You think that's the only reason I would…?

**意思：** 你觉得那就是我会……的唯一原因吗？（反问、否认狭窄动机）

> Wait, you think that's the only reason I would call you at this time of night?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:06:24–00:06:27

### 16. give sb. a little bit of credit

**意思：** 别把某人想得那么差／承认其好的一面，不是给贷款

> I mean, can you just give me... Give me a little bit of credit, okay?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:06:27–00:06:31

### 17. at worst

**意思：** 最差也不过……／顶多……（给出最坏程度）

> I am calling you tonight with quality PG phone conversation... PG-13 at worst.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:06:38–00:06:45

### 18. be swamped with work

**意思：** 工作多得忙不过来

> Uh, you're, uh, probably swamped with work anyways,

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:07:06–00:07:10

### 19. fall right into your lap

**意思：** 机会等主动送到面前，不费力便得到

> Uh, you're, uh, probably swamped with work anyways, what, with that big, fat, giant case falling right into your lap.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:07:06–00:07:14

### 20. steal sth. right out from under sb.

**意思：** 就在某人眼皮底下把东西／机会抢走

> The primo client your dipwad boss stole right out from under me.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:07:25–00:07:28

### 21. get away with + 金额

**意思：** 卷走／偷走多少钱；不是 get away with doing sth. 的逃脱惩罚义

> Hey, um, how much exactly did Kettleman get away with?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:07:44–00:07:49

### 22. have a right to know

**意思：** 有权知道（强调知情权）

> Uh, excuse me, but I'm a taxpayer in this county. I think I have a right to know.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:07:53–00:07:58

### 23. It's not like I'm… or anything.

**意思：** 我又不是……之类的（弱化或否认某种身份／关系）

> And besides, it's not like I'm his lawyer or anything.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:07:58–00:08:02

### 24. think out loud

**意思：** 把未定的想法随口说出来，不是已经作出决定

> I'm... I'm drunk. So, I'm just, uh... I'm thinking out loud. I'm... I'm up, spinning, uh... Spinning thoughts.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:08:49–00:09:00

### 25. Would you work with me here?

**意思：** 你能配合我一下／跟我一起找个办法吗？（争取协商）

> Would you work with me here?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:13:55–00:13:57

### 26. a captive audience

**意思：** 无法离开、只能听下去的听众（此处带玩笑）

> Well, you're kind of a captive audience.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:13:59–00:14:01

### 27. hear sb. out

**意思：** 先听某人把话说完，再作判断

> But hear me out. Wow.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:14:06–00:14:09

### 28. tell one sth. from another

**意思：** 分清这个和另一个；原句指连不同被告都分不清

> and you can't tell one defendant from another?!

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:14:57–00:15:00

### 29. You know the drill.

**意思：** 你知道流程／规矩的（熟悉、重复的做法）

> You know the drill... Money or the validation.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:15:57–00:15:59

### 30. I'm not making you do anything.

**意思：** 我没有强迫你做什么（否认自己在施加要求）

> I'm not making you do anything. Those are the rules.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:16:21–00:16:24

### 31. Whatever helps you sleep at night.

**意思：** 你觉得这样就问心无愧，那随你吧（此处讽刺自我开脱）

> Hey, whatever helps you sleep at night.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:16:25–00:16:27

### 32. No sign of sb./sth.

**意思：** 没有某人／某物的踪迹

> The place was ransacked. No sign of the family.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:17:53–00:17:55

### 33. in her time of need

**意思：** 在她需要帮助／处境困难的时候

> I'm just supporting a friend in her time of need.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:18:18–00:18:20

### 34. read tea leaves

**意思：** 根据零碎迹象推测情况；原本指以茶叶占卜

> I'm just reading tea leaves, same way I knew the Kettlemans needed a lawyer.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:18:23–00:18:26

### 35. You would… if you could…, right?

**意思：** 如果你能……，你会……的，对吧？（求证、施加期待）

> You would... Say something if you could help find them, right?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:18:41–00:18:46

### 36. The longer…, the more…

**意思：** 越……就越……；此处强调逗留造成额外解释负担

> The longer you stay here, the more explaining I have to do to Hamlin.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:18:52–00:18:56

### 37. You'll tell me if there's anything I can do?

**意思：** 有我能帮忙的地方，你会告诉我吧？（试探性提供帮助）

> Hey, you'll tell me if there's anything I can do?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:18:57–00:19:01

### 38. It's gonna work out.

**意思：** 事情会有办法／会顺利解决（安慰，不是锻炼）

> Everything is gonna be fine. It's gonna work out. A okay. It's gonna be fine.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:19:18–00:19:26

### 39. at your earliest convenience

**意思：** 请在你方便时尽快……（较正式，不是随时都行）

> Um, so call me at 146-8729 at your earliest convenience. But soon, please.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:20:16–00:20:26

### 40. Just to clarify,…

**意思：** 澄清一下……（纠正可能的误会）

> Uh... Just to clarify, look, I don't know anything. Uh, I have spoken to no one, and, uh, there are no rats on this ship.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:20:54–00:21:03

### 41. for the sake of everyone involved

**意思：** 为了所有相关的人着想

> But for the sake of everyone involved, I would just like to open a dialogue.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:21:03–00:21:10

### 42. open a dialogue

**意思：** 开始沟通，建立协商渠道（较正式）

> But for the sake of everyone involved, I would just like to open a dialogue.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:21:03–00:21:10

### 43. Time is of the essence.

**意思：** 时间至关重要，不能拖延（正式、强调紧迫）

> If I didn't say it before, time is of the essence on this matter, so, uh, I'm gonna hang up now... If you're trying to call.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:21:29–00:21:39

### 44. put out this fire

**意思：** 处理眼前危机／平息问题（比喻）

> I can put out this fire, if there is a fire.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:21:48–00:21:54

### 45. give sb. an earful

**意思：** 对某人说一大通；常指训斥／抱怨，此处指告知大量不利信息

> Uh, those two detectives... They just gave me an earful. And what they were telling me is... it's problematic.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:25:10–00:25:20

### 46. be on the same page

**意思：** 对情况／目标有一致理解

> I'm gonna pitch it back to you so I know we're on the same page.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:25:22–00:25:27

### 47. knock sth. down to sth.

**意思：** 把数额／程度降到……；此处是争取降低刑期

> I feel very good about knocking your sentence down to the minimum... 18 years.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:26:11–00:26:19

### 48. weigh in

**意思：** 参与讨论，发表意见

> Y... you want to tell me your thoughts and weigh in?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:26:41–00:26:46

### 49. a plan sb. can get behind

**意思：** 某人愿意支持的计划；get behind 指支持

> Does this sound like a plan that you can get behind?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:26:46–00:26:51

### 50. set sb. up

**意思：** 陷害某人／让某人背锅；此处不是帮忙安排约会或设备

> - You set me up. - I what? You gave my score to another crew, and now you're setting me up.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:26:58–00:27:05

### 51. Are you saying (that)…?

**意思：** 你是说……？（核实含义或表达怀疑）

> Are you saying that you had nothing to do with this?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:27:12–00:27:14

### 52. have nothing to do with sth.

**意思：** 与某事毫无关系；此处否认参与，不是没有事可做

> Are you saying that you had nothing to do with this?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:27:12–00:27:14

### 53. Go figure.

**意思：** 你说这怎么回事／真是让人想不通（此处带讽刺）

> Now here I am, under arrest. Go figure.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:28:11–00:28:16

### 54. Bad as in…?

**意思：** 你说的“糟”具体是指……？（as in 用来要求澄清含义）

> it's gonna be bad for you... really bad. Bad? Bad as in...

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:28:34–00:28:43

### 55. Give me a break.

**意思：** 得了吧／少来这套（此处表示不相信，不是请求休息）

> He... he doesn't know because he didn't do it. Ah, Christ. Give me a break.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:29:29–00:29:32

### 56. How are you so sure?

**意思：** 你为什么这么肯定？（追问判断依据）

> How are you so sure?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:29:51–00:29:53

### 57. Well, isn't that convenient?

**意思：** 可真方便啊／借口可真巧啊（此处讽刺）

> That'll take weeks, if we could even get a DNA sample to compare it to. Well, isn't that convenient?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:30:08–00:30:14

### 58. evidence that points to sb./sth.

**意思：** 指向某人／某种解释的证据

> Look, there are no other suspects. There's no evidence that points to anyone else.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:30:19–00:30:24

### 59. Mission accomplished.

**意思：** 目的达到了／任务完成；此处讽刺对方已使自己动摇

> All right, you wanted me to crack? Mission accomplished.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:31:48–00:31:53

### 60. do sth. all you want

**意思：** 你想怎么做都行；此处将许可限制在找回家人之后

> Advocate for the guy all you want after we get these people back safe. In the meantime, help us.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:31:59–00:32:06

### 61. It makes perfect sense.

**意思：** 完全说得通／很合乎道理

> - I... it makes perfect sense. - How does it make sense?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:32:44–00:32:47

### 62. throw sb. off

**意思：** 误导某人，使其判断偏离正轨

> They staged this to throw everyone off.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:33:02–00:33:04

### 63. put the word out

**意思：** 把消息传出去／通知大家

> You... you got to put the word out.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:33:09–00:33:12

### 64. You don't think it's possible (that)…?

**意思：** 你不觉得……有可能吗？（挑战对方的排除判断）

> You don't think it's possible I'm right?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:33:14–00:33:16

### 65. on the Q.T.

**意思：** 私下、秘密地（口语表达，不当作正式通知用语）

> I've got to tell you something on the Q.T., and promise not to be upset with me.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:33:47–00:33:51

### 66. No way am I doing sth.

**意思：** 我绝不……（强烈拒绝；No way 前置后倒装）

> No way am I making that promise.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:33:51–00:33:53

### 67. file that under + 类别

**意思：** 把那件事归为……（借档案分类来评价，常带幽默）

> I did, with the tube and the whole thing, which probably scared the living shit out of them, and they took off, which, you know, file that under "unintended consequences,"

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:34:18–00:34:27

### 68. or, better yet,…

**意思：** 或者，更好的办法是……（升级前一个建议）

> Now we have to find them. I mean... or, better yet, get the cops to let Varga go because right now my ass is on a highway to the danger zone.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:34:33–00:34:43

### 69. There's no way I'm gonna…

**意思：** 我绝不／不可能……（坚决排除某个行动）

> No, there's n... there's no way I'm gonna rat on this guy. I will never be safe.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:35:02–00:35:06

### 70. rat on sb.

**意思：** 告发／出卖某人（负面评价）

> No, there's n... there's no way I'm gonna rat on this guy. I will never be safe.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:35:02–00:35:06

### 71. be on the run

**意思：** 在逃／躲避追捕

> No, I have to convince the cops that I'm right, get them to stop looking at Nacho, and catch the Kettlemans on the run.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:35:06–00:35:12

### 72. be not exactly + 名词／形容词

**意思：** 算不上……／也不是多么……（此处委婉说他们不聪明）

> Well, you've met these people, right? They're... they're not exactly masterminds, right?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:35:15–00:35:19

### 73. get in on sth.

**意思：** 参与某件正在进行的事

> The FBI, too... I heard they're getting in on this.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:35:24–00:35:26

### 74. have clout with sb.

**意思：** 对某些人有影响力，能说得上话

> He has clout with these people.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:35:34–00:35:36

### 75. It's sb.'s call.

**意思：** 由某人拍板／这是某人的决定

> It's Hamlin's call, and Hamlin will never agree.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:35:46–00:35:49

### 76. Even if it were up to me,…

**意思：** 即使由我决定，……（假设让步；此处仍表示不能答应）

> And even if it were up to me, you know I couldn't.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:35:49–00:35:53

### 77. make sb. see reason

**意思：** 让某人听进道理、理智一些

> I'm gonna go talk to Nacho. I'll try to make him see reason.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:36:03–00:36:08

### 78. I don't think you want to be doing that.

**意思：** 我觉得你最好别那么做（此处是含蓄警告）

> Hey. I'm gonna... I'm gonna park right here. I don't think you want to be doing that.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:37:17–00:37:23

### 79. have sb. dead to rights

**意思：** 掌握足以证明某人有错／犯罪的证据（美式；原对白的主张）

> Yeah, county treasurer embezzled funds. I read the paper. Yeah, well, we got this gangbanger dead to rights.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:37:51–00:37:55

### 80. be on board

**意思：** 愿意参与／支持某个方案

> So, what do you say? You on board? Help us do some good.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:38:03–00:38:07

### 81. let sth. go

**意思：** 不再追究／放过某件事；此处是不追究冲突

> Now, this gentleman is willing to let this go, not press charges, if you help us out.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:38:22–00:38:25

### 82. press charges

**意思：** 提出刑事控告／要求追究刑事责任（法律用语，不等于个人决定检方一定起诉）

> Now, this gentleman is willing to let this go, not press charges, if you help us out.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:38:22–00:38:25

### 83. sound like a broken record

**意思：** 像坏唱片一样反复说同样的话（原文用 definition of a broken record）

> Wait, guys, I'm the definition of a broken record on this one. Nacho is "not-cho" man, comprendo?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:38:28–00:38:34

### 84. Do your worst.

**意思：** 你尽管使出最狠的手段吧（强硬挑衅）

> You're gonna keep playing that line, it's gonna go rough. You know what? Do your worst.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:38:38–00:38:44

### 85. I thought you had our backs.

**意思：** 我还以为你会支持／保护我们（失望或责怪）

> What are you doing, buddy? I thought you had our backs.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:39:00–00:39:02

### 86. heavy on sth.

**意思：** 多放点某种东西；此处要水果礼篮多放菠萝

> When you realize how wrong you are about all this, I'll take an edible arrangement as a "sorry." Heavy on the pineapple.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:39:18–00:39:24

### 87. let sb. off the hook

**意思：** 放过某人，使其免于责任／困境

> How come you let me off the hook back there?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:39:30–00:39:32

### 88. quit while you're ahead

**意思：** 见好就收，不要继续冒险把优势弄丢

> Why don't you quit while you're ahead and go on your way?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:39:34–00:39:36

### 89. I refuse to believe (that)…

**意思：** 我不愿／不能接受……这种解释（明确否认）

> No, I refuse to believe it's because you have something resembling a heart inside your body.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:39:36–00:39:40

### 90. play devil's advocate

**意思：** 暂时站在相反立场提出质疑，不一定真持反对意见；原文省略 play

> Devil's advocate... like the cops said, uh, the Kettlemans' cars are still at their house. There's no record of them leaving.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:40:08–00:40:14

### 91. Odds are,…

**意思：** 很可能……／大概率……

> Odds are, they didn't get out of their neighborhood.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:40:17–00:40:19

### 92. Come again?

**意思：** 你说什么？／再说一遍？（没听清或惊讶）

> What? What? They... come again?

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:40:19–00:40:21

### 93. skip town

**意思：** 偷偷离开城镇／跑路，常为逃避麻烦

> This bookie disappeared after the super bowl... Cowboys/steelers... took $6 million in bets and skipped town when things didn't go his way.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:40:31–00:40:41

### 94. Things didn't go his way.

**意思：** 事情没有按他希望的方向发展

> This bookie disappeared after the super bowl... Cowboys/steelers... took $6 million in bets and skipped town when things didn't go his way.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:40:31–00:40:41

### 95. It's human nature to want to…

**意思：** 想要……是人之常情（用于解释动机）

> Now, that's what everybody expects. It's human nature to want to stay close to home,

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:40:57–00:41:03

### 96. I don't know about you, but…

**意思：** 不知道你怎么想，但我……（引出自己的偏好／立场）

> Oh, hey, I don't know about you, but I would love a little "John Jacob Jingleheimer Schmidt."

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:45:03–00:45:08

### 97. talk sth. through

**意思：** 把事情详细谈清楚／讨论到有结果；不是仅随便聊聊

> Uh, look, could... please, could we just - talk this through, okay? - It's not an option.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:45:39–00:45:44

### 98. It's not an option.

**意思：** 这不是可以选择的办法／这事没得商量（此处拒绝继续谈）

> Uh, look, could... please, could we just - talk this through, okay? - It's not an option.

**来源：** S01E03 · 00:45:39–00:45:44


## S01E04

### 1. It's a given (that)…

**意思：** ……是理所当然／无需再说的；a given 是既定事实

> It's a given you don't do it in her ear.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:00:23–00:00:25

### 2. I know… and everything, but…

**意思：** 我知道……什么的，但……（先承认，再表达不同看法）

> I'm sorry, bro. I know it's yours and everything, but... - It's not even 2:00. - Yeah, it does kind of suck.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:00:45–00:00:52

### 3. I never did catch your name.

**意思：** 我还没听到／记住你的名字；委婉补问，did 表强调

> Bro, I never did catch your name.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:01:18–00:01:20

### 4. There's got to be…

**意思：** 这里肯定有……／一定得有……；原例作肯定推测

> There's got to be at least a thousand bucks in here, man.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:01:38–00:01:40

### 5. have dibs on sth.

**意思：** 先声明某物归自己／有优先权（口语，常用于抢先选）

> I got dibs on that watch, man.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:04:06–00:04:09

### 6. split sth. up

**意思：** 把某物分开分配；此处是分钱与手表

> So, how do you want to split this up?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:04:41–00:04:42

### 7. I'll tell you what…

**意思：** 这样吧……（提出办法／交易条件）

> I'll tell you what... you keep the cash. - I'll just keep the watch. - But it's worth like 3 grand, man.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:04:42–00:04:48

### 8. sweeten the pot

**意思：** 加码，使交易／奖励更有吸引力

> I... sweeten the pot. I got, $5, $20, $40, $60, $80... 580 bucks. Add that to the $1,000... It's more than half.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:04:57–00:05:06

### 9. We're good, right?

**意思：** 咱们没问题／谈妥了，对吧？（也可确认关系无芥蒂）

> No, I don't... I don't know. But we're good, right? Yeah? We good?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:05:09–00:05:13

### 10. a license to print money

**意思：** 很容易持续赚钱的生意／办法（夸张）

> It's like a license to print money.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:05:43–00:05:45

### 11. It just came out.

**意思：** 就那么脱口而出了／顺口说出来了

> Just something in my brain, you know? It just came out. Too much?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:05:53–00:05:57

### 12. Too much?

**意思：** 是不是有点过头了？（简短征询力度是否合适）

> It just came out. Too much?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:05:54–00:05:57

### 13. That's about all.

**意思：** 也就这样了／仅此而已（限制价值或程度）

> It's good for making beer money. That's about all.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:06:13–00:06:17

### 14. No one has to know.

**意思：** 没必要让别人知道／可以瞒住别人（不是没人知道）

> Please, okay? No one has to know. I mean, you could just pretend you didn't find us.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:06:43–00:06:48

### 15. round sb. up

**意思：** 搜捕／集中带走某人；此处指警方抓来嫌疑人

> The cops are investigating this as a kidnapping. They already rounded up some poor suspect. A working-class guy. Father... I think.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:06:59–00:07:10

### 16. Could this have been…?

**意思：** 这会不会就是……？（猜测过去的人或事）

> Could this have been the person in the van?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:07:17–00:07:20

### 17. What if…?

**意思：** 要是……呢？／有没有可能……？（原例提出另一种解释）

> What if the man they arrested Is the one we were warned about? Yeah, maybe he's not so innocent?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:07:20–00:07:28

### 18. I wouldn't know about any of that.

**意思：** 那些事情我可不知道／不便作判断（此处回避表态）

> I wouldn't know about any of that.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:07:46–00:07:48

### 19. I can assure you (that)…

**意思：** 我可以向你保证……（明确安抚或确认）

> I can assure you that you and your family are safe. I mean, the police are all over this thing, so...

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:07:50–00:07:55

### 20. be all over sth.

**意思：** 全力关注／处理某事；此处说警方正在紧盯此事

> I can assure you that you and your family are safe. I mean, the police are all over this thing, so...

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:07:50–00:07:55

### 21. risk doing sth.

**意思：** 冒做某事的风险；此处是不愿冒失去一切的风险

> If they think I ran away, it'll look like I did something wrong. We can't risk losing everything just because of some teensy mistake.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:08:07–00:08:14

### 22. What were you hoping would happen?

**意思：** 你本来希望会发生什么？（追问预期结果）

> What... what were you hoping would happen? I mean, before I found you, what were you planning to do?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:08:16–00:08:21

### 23. be in the process of doing sth.

**意思：** 正在做某事的过程中（较中性正式）

> Well, we were in the process of, you know, working that out... - We were working that out. - When you, um...

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:08:23–00:08:28

### 24. work sth. out

**意思：** 想出／商定具体办法；此处还在考虑接下来怎么做

> Well, we were in the process of, you know, working that out... - We were working that out. - When you, um...

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:08:23–00:08:28

### 25. make it work

**意思：** 想办法让事情行得通／把问题处理好

> Kim Wexler is very good. She'll make it work.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:08:47–00:08:50

### 26. It's not a bug, it's a feature.

**意思：** 这不是缺陷，而是特点／优势（常带幽默地重新解读问题）

> Well, like they say in Silicon Valley, "it's not a bug... it's a feature!"

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:08:59–00:09:03

### 27. a bargaining chip

**意思：** 谈判筹码，用来争取条件的优势

> Did you ever hear of a bargaining chip?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:09:04–00:09:06

### 28. the mother of all + 复数名词

**意思：** 最强／最大／最夸张的……（夸张口语）

> What we have right here is the mother of all bargaining chips.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:09:06–00:09:10

### 29. Just because… doesn't mean…

**意思：** 不能仅因为……就认为……；否定由某事实推出结论

> And, really, just because you're salaried, doesn't mean you don't deserve overtime.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:09:30–00:09:34

### 30. That's only fair.

**意思：** 这才公平／这不过是应得的（为要求辩护）

> I think that's only fair. I mean, really, that's what this is about, right? - Fairness. - Fairness, right.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:09:34–00:09:39

### 31. be right up there with sth.

**意思：** 与某事同一等级／同样突出；此处讽刺对方的类比

> If you want to talk about legal, slavery... That used to be legal. Human slavery. So... Yeah, this is right up there with that.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:09:41–00:09:52

### 32. How hard is that?

**意思：** 这能有多难？（反问，可能施加压力）

> Please, didn't you say that you wanted to help us? So just pretend you never saw the money. How hard is that?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:10:11–00:10:17

### 33. be chock-full of sth.

**意思：** 满是／塞满了某物（口语）

> Look... I know that HHM is shiny and it's slick. It's chock-full of lawyers and, compared to them, I'm like a... A kiddy lemonade stand trying to compete with Wal-Mart.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:10:41–00:10:52

### 34. But here's the thing…

**意思：** 但关键在于……／不过有一点……（引出要点）

> But here's the thing... What are you gonna get from me That you're not gonna get from those other guys? Passion, commitment.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:10:52–00:11:01

### 35. What are you gonna get from me that…?

**意思：** 你能从我这里得到什么，而从别人那里得不到？（强调差异价值）

> What are you gonna get from me That you're not gonna get from those other guys? Passion, commitment.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:10:55–00:11:01

### 36. Ask yourself this…

**意思：** 你问问自己这个问题……（引导对方重新判断）

> Ask yourself this... who found you? I don't see Howard Hamlin... ruining his $300 Gucci loafers out here.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:11:01–00:11:09

### 37. morning, noon, or night

**意思：** 无论白天晚上／任何时间

> If you're with me, you're my number-one client. Morning, noon, or night, you call me... I'm there.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:11:09–00:11:14

### 38. make the paper

**意思：** 上报纸，被报纸报道；paper 此处是报纸

> Did it make the paper? - What? - Did it make the paper? Did what make the paper? Okay, well, probably missed the deadline, but check it out tomorrow morning... front page.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:12:07–00:12:18

### 39. safe and sound

**意思：** 平安无事，安然无恙

> The Kettlemans, all four of them... Safe and sound.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:12:18–00:12:21

### 40. You called it.

**意思：** 被你说中了／你预测得对

> - Looks like you called it. - Yeah. Camping... you know, pretty much right in their own backyard. Well... Five miles above it.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:12:21–00:12:30

### 41. break my heart

**意思：** 让我伤心／心碎；此处对罪犯不聪明的说法带玩笑

> You assume that criminals are gonna be smarter than they are. I don't know. It kind of breaks my heart a little.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:12:41–00:12:50

### 42. why on earth…?

**意思：** 到底为什么……？（加强疑惑或不满）

> Whoa, whoa. Why is my client in handcuffs? This man is innocent. He's a victim. why... why on earth would he be cuffed?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:13:25–00:13:33

### 43. push your luck

**意思：** 得寸进尺／进一步冒险，可能把好运耗尽

> You really don't want to push your luck with me, scumbag. You got eyes on you, asshole. One little slip... just one.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:13:50–00:13:57

### 44. make us square

**意思：** 让我们两清／互不相欠；square 不是正方形

> I believe I did more than what you asked of me, so, that would make us square, yes?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:14:07–00:14:15

### 45. You expect me to believe…?

**意思：** 你指望我相信……？（强烈怀疑）

> Camping? You expect me to believe that shit?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:14:19–00:14:24

### 46. run sth. by sb.

**意思：** 把想法／方案告诉某人，听取意见

> They decide to go camping Right after I run my little offer by you?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:14:29–00:14:32

### 47. between you and me

**意思：** 私下说／只告诉你（引出不公开的话）

> And between you and me, They're pretty rash when it comes to the decision-making. I mean, they're not really the plan-ahead types.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:14:52–00:14:59

### 48. I'd… if I were you.

**意思：** 我要是你就会……（原例是警告，非友善建议）

> Yeah, I'd cut the cute attitude right about now If I were you.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:14:59–00:15:03

### 49. Not to mention…

**意思：** 更不用说……／还有……（补充进一步理由）

> You didn't need any help getting caught, okay? The neighbor ID'd you. You were sloppy. Any trouble you might have... that's on you. Not to mention the blood in your van.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:15:21–00:15:33

### 50. You have no idea…

**意思：** 你根本不知道……有多……（强调对方不了解的程度）

> You have no idea the tap-dance I had to give those cops To get you out of here. You gave them probable cause out the wazoo.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:15:37–00:15:45

### 51. out the wazoo

**意思：** 多得不得了（粗俗口语）；此处是证据／理由很多

> You gave them probable cause out the wazoo.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:15:41–00:15:45

### 52. round sth. off to + 数字

**意思：** 把某数取整／凑整到……；此处将工时按 20 小时算

> $950 an hour. round it off to 20 hours.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:17:11–00:17:17

### 53. I have the very thing.

**意思：** 我正好有你要的东西（very 强调恰好）

> Okay, I'm gonna need your finest sea island cotton with a white club collar and french cuffs. I have the very thing.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:18:29–00:18:37

### 54. I was picturing something in…

**意思：** 我原本设想的是……样式／颜色（描述想要的效果）

> Yeah, I was, you know, picturing something in a... - A light-blue knit. -

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:18:40–00:18:45

### 55. It's not even close.

**意思：** 差得远／根本不像（否定比较）

> So, um, what do you think? "Strawberry Fields Forever"? No. Are you crazy? It's not even close. - Yes, it is. Look. - No, no. It's way more "Sassafras Glow". Way more.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:19:38–00:19:54

### 56. back sb. up

**意思：** 支持某人的说法／替某人作证；此处请求旁人帮腔

> Use your eyes... Strawberry Fields Forever. Ladies, back me up. Hey. Is it... Sassafras Glow. - Am I right? - No. Maybe "Rhapsody in Blonde," but... Yeah, see? They agree with me.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:19:54–00:20:07

### 57. Whatever happened to…?

**意思：** ……不是一直这么说的吗？／……怎么不作数了？（质疑原则被忘记）

> Whatever happened to "the customer is always right"?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:20:07–00:20:09

### 58. Big deal. / No biggie.

**意思：** 有什么大不了／没什么（前者常不屑，后者随意轻描淡写）

> Big deal. I'll wash it out in the morning. - No biggie. Geez. - It's bleach. - It doesn't wash out. - What? It's permanent?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:20:13–00:20:20

### 59. go with sth.

**意思：** 选用某个方案／风格；不是陪某人一起走

> Change of plan, ladies. I want to go with a simple curling-iron job, okay? Give me, like, ringlets... You know, on top, like Tony Curtis in "Spartacus."

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:20:23–00:20:35

### 60. be up for sth.

**意思：** 愿意／有兴致参加某事；原例邀请看电影

> Anyway, "The Thing" is playing downtown on Saturday. Thought you might be up for some Kurt Russell action.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:21:23–00:21:31

### 61. I owe you one.

**意思：** 我欠你一个人情（不一定指具体钱款）

> I owe you one, so why don't we just grab some dinner first?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:21:31–00:21:34

### 62. My treat.

**意思：** 我请客，我买单

> I owe you one, so why don't we just grab some dinner first? My treat.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:21:31–00:21:36

### 63. If not, can you… anyway?

**意思：** 如果不行，你还是能……吗？（保留另一项请求）

> And... You know, if not, can you give me a call anyway? I really want to talk to you.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:21:36–00:21:43

### 64. If it's okay with you,…

**意思：** 如果你同意／不介意，我就……（先征求许可）

> If it's okay with you, I think I should try to talk them into hiring a PR firm. Certainly for the next couple of weeks.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:22:05–00:22:12

### 65. talk sb. into doing sth.

**意思：** 说服某人做某事（不是单纯与其谈论）

> If it's okay with you, I think I should try to talk them into hiring a PR firm. Certainly for the next couple of weeks.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:22:05–00:22:12

### 66. Am I crazy here?

**意思：** 是我想多了吗／看错了吗？（求证自己的判断）

> That's... I mean, that's my suit, right? - Am I crazy here? - No, you're not crazy. Look at the logo. That's our logo.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:23:00–00:23:11

### 67. That's one way to put it.

**意思：** 可以这么说吧（此处含保留，暗示另一种更负面的说法）

> I really... h-h-he's, you know, a free spirit. Yeah, that's one way to put it.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:23:18–00:23:25

### 68. give sb. the third degree

**意思：** 盘问某人；原文用 This isn't the third degree 否认在审问

> This isn't the third degree, Kim. I really don't care one way or the other.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:23:41–00:23:44

### 69. I don't care one way or the other.

**意思：** 无论怎样我都不在意／哪一种都无所谓

> This isn't the third degree, Kim. I really don't care one way or the other.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:23:41–00:23:44

### 70. force my hand

**意思：** 逼得我不得不采取行动（通常并非本来想做的）

> He's really forcing my hand here.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:23:49–00:23:52

### 71. Am I interrupting?

**意思：** 我有没有打扰你？（进入谈话或工作时试探）

> Am I interrupting? Not at all. Perfect timing. Come on.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:24:19–00:24:24

### 72. free of charge

**意思：** 免费，不收费用

> Sit. Have a massage, free of charge.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:24:26–00:24:29

### 73. one of the perks of sth.

**意思：** 某事／生活方式带来的好处之一（附带福利）

> I get this free every night... Just one of the perks of salon living. The others being... Free cucumber water, obviously.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:25:07–00:25:15

### 74. This won't end well for you.

**意思：** 这事对你不会有好结果（警告／威胁）

> Well, his head didn't actually explode, but, you know, pretty damn pissed, like "this won't end well for you" pissed.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:25:53–00:25:58

### 75. What the hell is with…?

**意思：** ……到底是怎么回事？（粗俗，强烈不满）

> What the hell is with that billboard? I know you have a problem with Hamlin, but I... I just don't get your angle here.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:26:06–00:26:15

### 76. make this personal

**意思：** 把争执变成针对个人／夹带私人恩怨

> I mean... Why are you making this personal? It's not personal? It is so personal. It is completely and totally personal.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:26:23–00:26:33

### 77. fire the first shot

**意思：** 先挑起冲突／率先发难（比喻）

> Hey, all right, now, he fired the first shot, okay? Trying to keep me from using my name, my own name, Kim?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:27:02–00:27:08

### 78. come after sb.

**意思：** 找某人麻烦／针对、追究某人；此处威胁法律行动

> If Hamlin wants to come after me, he knows where I am. I'll be ready, guns blazing.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:27:43–00:27:48

### 79. with guns blazing

**意思：** 火力全开／强势迎战（原文省略 with，比喻）

> If Hamlin wants to come after me, he knows where I am. I'll be ready, guns blazing.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:27:43–00:27:48

### 80. ply my trade

**意思：** 从事自己的职业／做本行生意（较正式）

> Your honor, I'm a humble solo practitioner, merely trying to ply my trade In an aggressive and evolving marketplace.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:28:43–00:28:51

### 81. There are only so many…

**意思：** ……的数量毕竟有限（强调选择有限，不是数量很多）

> It... there are only so many fonts out there. Does Mr. Hamlin outright own them all?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:29:03–00:29:07

### 82. in concert with sth.

**意思：** 与某物一起／协同发挥作用（较正式）

> No, but we've been using this particular font for 12 years now, and it, in concert with our tri-rectangle graphic and Hamlindigo Blue, constitutes a trademarked brand identifier.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:29:07–00:29:18

### 83. Back up.

**意思：** 等等，回到刚才那一点（此处不是让车倒退）

> Whoa, whoa. Back up. Hamlindigo blue? Yes. That is our trademarked name.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:29:18–00:29:24

### 84. So I can't… now?

**意思：** 所以现在连……都不行了？（反问，表达不满）

> So I can't advertise under my own name now? I'm to be penalized 'cause I happen to share a name with one of HHM's partners? You can't take my name from me.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:29:48–00:29:57

### 85. be within your rights to do sth.

**意思：** 有权／在合理权利范围内做某事

> All right, yes, you are within your rights to advertise using your own name.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:30:18–00:30:21

### 86. for your own gain

**意思：** 为了自己获利／谋取个人利益

> However, in my estimation, the billboard clearly and intentionally duplicates elements of the Hamlin, Hamlin, McGill logo. You're actively copying their established brand for your own gain.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:30:21–00:30:34

### 87. Wise up.

**意思：** 清醒点／明白点吧（直接，可带责备）

> I don't see any other reasonable explanation. - Your honor, I... - Jimmy, Jimmy. Wise up. The billboard must come down within 48 hours.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:30:34–00:30:42

### 88. It's right up your alley.

**意思：** 这正合你兴趣／是你擅长的事（美式口语）

> Yeah, but it's investigative journalism at its best. I-it's right up your alley.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:30:52–00:30:56

### 89. sth. at its best

**意思：** 某事最出色／最充分展现特点的样子

> Yeah, but it's investigative journalism at its best. I-it's right up your alley.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:30:52–00:30:56

### 90. a hot tip

**意思：** 有价值的内幕线索／独家消息（不只是建议）

> Can I get the news desk, please? I got a hot tip. Don't you want to be the station that brought down big law? You could be like the reporters that brought down big tobacco. Look, I'm talking Woodward and Bernstein here.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:31:16–00:31:27

### 91. a miscarriage of justice

**意思：** 司法不公／错误的司法结果（严肃用语，原说话人如此定性）

> Well, it's a miscarriage of justice, And I think your viewers would be very, very interested And, grateful to you for exposing this injustice.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:31:31–00:31:40

### 92. a pillar of the community

**意思：** 社区中受尊敬、具有重要作用的人／组织

> No, yes, HHM is a pillar of the community, yes.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:31:45–00:31:49

### 93. pull yourself up by your bootstraps

**意思：** 靠自己的努力改善处境（比喻）；原例用于塑造自力更生形象

> Well, at least consider the human-interest angle here. Are you kidding? It's... lawyer pulls himself up by his bootstraps, Only to be ground under the heel of the old-money megafirm?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:31:57–00:32:07

### 94. May I suggest (that)…?

**意思：** 容我建议……？（礼貌地提出具体改动）

> Fine, may I suggest you widen the frame to include... The billboard is the story, okay? No, I'm also the story, so... Got to get me in there.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:33:11–00:33:20

### 95. No offense, but…

**意思：** 无意冒犯，但……（缓冲批评，不保证对方不会被冒犯）

> You know, no offense to your minutes of experience, But I'd like to see what we got before we do this, okay?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:33:21–00:33:26

### 96. put myself through school

**意思：** 自己挣钱支付学费并完成学业

> Like most Americans, I'm a self-made man. I put myself through law school.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:34:29–00:34:35

### 97. scrimp and save

**意思：** 省吃俭用，努力攒钱

> Well, I-i scrimped and I saved, and finally, I was able to buy one billboard... A tiny foray into advertising for my fledgling law firm.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:34:52–00:35:03

### 98. side with sb.

**意思：** 站在／支持某人一边；此处指法院裁决偏向哪一方

> They said that I was hurting their business. They're rich. They're powerful. I'm just... One man. So, who do you think the courts sided with? So now my little billboard comes down.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:35:17–00:35:34

### 99. one sth. at a time

**意思：** 一次一个／一步一步来；原例每次爬一级梯子

> Not looking down. One rung at a time. Not looking down.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:36:30–00:36:37

### 100. Took you long enough.

**意思：** 你可算……了／怎么这么久才来（不耐烦或打趣，省略 It）

> You okay? Took you long enough.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:38:05–00:38:09

### 101. be not a big fan of sth.

**意思：** 不太喜欢某事／某物（较委婉，原例说怕高）

> I was scared, yes. I mean, I'm not really a big fan of heights... Let me tell you.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:38:20–00:38:25

### 102. buy this

**意思：** 相信这套说法，不是购买东西

> This whole thing was a publicity stunt. It's got to be. I think that's kind of all anybody wants when they're in trouble. Of course I didn't think... You don't think anyone's gonna actually buy this?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:38:36–00:38:44

### 103. It's hard to say.

**意思：** 很难说／不好判断（保留不确定性）

> You don't think anyone's gonna actually buy this? It's hard to say. People love a hero.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:38:42–00:38:49

### 104. take it as it comes

**意思：** 事情来了再应对／顺其自然地处理

> You take it as it comes, right? The universe put me in that spot at that moment.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:38:49–00:38:53

### 105. Who am I to say no?

**意思：** 我又有什么理由／资格拒绝呢？（修辞性反问）

> You take it as it comes, right? The universe put me in that spot at that moment. Who am I to say no?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:38:49–00:38:54

### 106. ring off the hook

**意思：** 电话响个不停，来电接连不断

> Hey, big day. My phone's ringing off the hook. I have three consults before lunchtime... three.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:41:12–00:41:19

### 107. The worm has turned.

**意思：** 原文借用：局面扭转；通常指长期受欺压者开始反抗，不泛化为万能“转运”说法

> The worm has turned.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:41:22–00:41:25

### 108. To what do you credit sth.?

**意思：** 你把某个变化／成果归功于什么？（较正式；口语 What do you credit sth. to?）

> So, to what do you credit this sudden change?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:41:27–00:41:30

### 109. keep plugging

**意思：** 继续坚持、努力干下去（原文 plugging 未接宾语）

> Well, I got to admit... you were right. How so? Well, you told me to keep plugging and, you know... do good work, and the clients will come.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:41:30–00:41:39

### 110. elbow grease

**意思：** 卖力干活／投入体力劳动，不是润滑油

> I mean, hey, just took some elbow grease and clean living... People noticed.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:42:19–00:42:25

### 111. I knew you had it in you.

**意思：** 我就知道你有这个能力／潜力

> I knew you had it in you.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:42:26–00:42:28

### 112. I don't know what to tell you.

**意思：** 我不知道还能怎么说／给你什么解释（可能无奈或回避）

> Yeah. I didn't see it outside. Wasn't out there? It's always out there. I didn't see it. Maybe they forgot to deliver it. Maybe some kids grabbed it. Because if there's one thing kids love, it's local print journalism. I don't know what to tell you.

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:42:39–00:42:54

### 113. tide sb. over

**意思：** 暂时满足所需、撑到之后；此处问另一份报纸能否先凑合读

> You know, I know Albuquerque is a hub of global intrigue, but you think that'll tide you over?

**来源：** S01E04 · 00:43:00–00:43:05


## S01E05

### 1. All we want to do is…

**意思：** 我们只是想……（限定目的，减轻戒心）

> All we want to do is talk.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:01:40–00:01:42

### 2. I'd prefer not to.

**意思：** 我更愿意不这么做（委婉拒绝）

> I'd, uh, I'd prefer not to, officers.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:01:45–00:01:48

### 3. be exposed to sth.

**意思：** 接触／暴露于某事物（原例电磁刺激）

> Uh, I can't go outside or be exposed to what's out there.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:01:53–00:01:57

### 4. As I'm sure you know,…

**意思：** 想必你也知道……（引出共同知识，可能带施压）

> As I'm sure you know, in the state of New Mexico, there is a two-part test to determine whether police officers have an objectively reasonable basis...

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:02:20–00:02:28

### 5. I can't emphasize that enough.

**意思：** 这点怎么强调都不为过

> I can't emphasize that enough. Deal?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:03:58–00:04:01

### 6. be in the shop

**意思：** 车在维修店检修（美式口语）

> Well, my Mercedes is in the shop.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:05:23–00:05:24

### 7. put yourself out there

**意思：** 主动让别人看见自己／冒险展示自己

> You ain't afraid to put yourself out there.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:05:39–00:05:41

### 8. get ahead

**意思：** 取得进展、出人头地（此处经济和事业上）

> It's a sign a good man can't get ahead today.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:05:58–00:06:02

### 9. poke sb.'s nose into sth.

**意思：** 插手、干涉某事（带不满）

> the I.N.S. poking their big, fat nose into every mother-loving hire.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:06:28–00:06:34

### 10. clear your calendar

**意思：** 腾出日程、取消别的安排

> Jim, I think you are just the lawyer I need, 'cause I got me a case... Major... I say, a major case. Are you ready to clear your calendar?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:06:43–00:06:54

### 11. I'm all ears.

**意思：** 我洗耳恭听／尽管说吧

> Ricky, I'm all ears.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:06:57–00:07:00

### 12. I'm your man.

**意思：** 这事找我就对了／我能帮你办

> I'm your man.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:07:28–00:07:30

### 13. end up doing sth. / end up in sth.

**意思：** 最终落到某种结果／处境；此处可能一路打到最高法院

> We could end up in the supreme court... I'm talking thousands of man-hours... I mean, years of effort.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:07:49–00:07:56

### 14. Bring them on.

**意思：** 让他们来吧／尽管来（自信迎战）

> I say bring 'em on.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:08:01–00:08:04

### 15. talk turkey

**意思：** 认真、坦率地谈实际问题（美式口语；此处谈价）

> Let's talk Turkey. What's your rate?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:08:04–00:08:07

### 16. The hell with sth.

**意思：** 管它呢／别管……了（粗口，抛开某事）

> The hell with hourly.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:08:16–00:08:18

### 17. have sb. on retainer

**意思：** 付预聘费，聘某人随时提供专业服务

> I want you on retainer.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:08:18–00:08:20

### 18. up front

**意思：** 预先支付；此处先付一半费用

> How about $1 million even... $500,000 up front and $500,000 when we're done?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:08:20–00:08:29

### 19. get in on the ground floor

**意思：** 在起步阶段参与，以争取先机

> Son... You are getting in on the ground floor.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:09:36–00:09:40

### 20. Must have been…, huh?

**意思：** 一定挺……的吧？（推测对方经历，邀请认同）

> God, what was it like climbing that billboard? Must have been pretty scary, huh?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:10:13–00:10:17

### 21. My hat is off to sb.

**意思：** 我佩服／敬重某人（致敬）

> Well, my hat is off to you.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:10:24–00:10:26

### 22. I feel silly asking this, but…

**意思：** 这么问有点不好意思，但……（缓冲请求）

> Uh, I feel silly asking this. Yeah. But before I show you my invention, would you mind signing a non-disclosure agreement?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:10:38–00:10:44

### 23. Would you mind doing sth.?

**意思：** 你介意做……吗？（礼貌请求）

> would you mind signing a non-disclosure agreement?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:10:42–00:10:44

### 24. get your hands on sth.

**意思：** 弄到／拿到某物（往往想要或不容易得到）

> If Fisher-price or Playskool ever got their hands on this...

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:10:51–00:10:53

### 25. No sense doing sth.

**意思：** 没必要／没意义做……（口语省略 There's no）

> and, well, no sense getting all technical, but it's all about positive reinforcement.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:11:33–00:11:38

### 26. It's all about sth.

**意思：** 关键就在于……／核心是……

> but it's all about positive reinforcement.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:11:35–00:11:38

### 27. do your business

**意思：** 上厕所、排便（委婉说法；不是做生意）

> And when you sit down to do your business, this is how he works.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:11:40–00:11:45

### 28. You get the picture.

**意思：** 你明白是怎么回事了吧（无需继续细说）

> Anyway, it goes on from there. You get the picture.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:12:26–00:12:29

### 29. I'm not saying (that)…, but…

**意思：** 我不是说……（先限定自己的立场；原例接解释）

> Look, I'm not... I'm not saying this thing won't make you rich. I mean, some of your wealthier pacific rim nations... They'll love this... the crazy bastards.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:12:50–00:12:59

### 30. Look how that turned out.

**意思：** 看看后来结果怎样（以结果提醒／反驳）

> Viagra was originally invented to treat hypertension. Look how that turned out.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:13:06–00:13:11

### 31. I hope you do…

**意思：** 我还真希望你……（do 加强愿望；此处挖苦）

> Hey, you know what? I hope you do make a fortune, 'cause Chandler's gonna need it to help pay for his therapy!

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:13:19–00:13:26

### 32. stay put

**意思：** 待在原地别动

> No, no. You just stay put.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:14:30–00:14:32

### 33. I'll be right over.

**意思：** 我马上就过去（去对方所在处）

> I'll be right over.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:14:32–00:14:34

### 34. Where were we?

**意思：** 刚才说到哪里了？（恢复话题）

> Now, where were we?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:15:18–00:15:19

### 35. no matter what

**意思：** 不管怎样／无论发生什么

> And no matter what, I want the towheaded twins to go to Reverend Lawrence's grandson.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:15:44–00:15:51

### 36. Don't you mean…?

**意思：** 你是想说……吧？（求证、纠正）

> Um, don't you mean Reverend Hanes? 'Cause Reverend Lawrence's grandson is going to get the, uh, lute-playing angel, 'cause he was in the choir.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:15:51–00:16:00

### 37. And here I thought…

**意思：** 我还以为……呢（事实推翻原先判断，常带调侃）

> Very sharp, Mr. McGill. And here I thought all lawyers were idiots.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:16:00–00:16:06

### 38. be in short supply

**意思：** 稀缺、不足

> Moxie is in such short supply these days.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:16:31–00:16:33

### 39. pride yourself on sth.

**意思：** 以某事为傲

> Well, I pride myself on my moxie.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:16:35–00:16:37

### 40. bring up sth.

**意思：** 提起某事（此处收费这一敏感话题）

> Now, I hate to do this, but I do need to bring up my payment.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:16:37–00:16:41

### 41. I hate to do this, but…

**意思：** 我也不想这样，可是……（铺垫难开口的要求）

> Now, I hate to do this, but I do need to bring up my payment. Um and I'm sorry, but I can't accept S&H green stamps.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:16:37–00:16:46

### 42. I can't help it if…

**意思：** 如果……也不能怪我／我也没办法

> I can't help it if your toes are too small.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:17:51–00:17:54

### 43. How does it go again?

**意思：** 那个具体怎么说／怎么来着？（请对方再提醒）

> So... how's it go again?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:18:02–00:18:07

### 44. do something about sth.

**意思：** 采取行动处理／改善某事

> Yeah, well, we should definitely do something about that.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:18:38–00:18:41

### 45. make a living at sth.

**意思：** 靠做某事谋生

> Seriously, don't ever try to make a living at this.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:18:44–00:18:46

### 46. stick with sth.

**意思：** 继续做／坚持某事（此处建议留在本行）

> Stick with the law.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:18:47–00:18:48

### 47. light the world on fire

**意思：** 做出轰动成绩（原例反讽自己并未大展身手）

> Yeah, because I'm really... Lighting the world on fire in that department.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:18:48–00:18:53

### 48. have a future in sth.

**意思：** 在某个领域有发展前景

> Maybe you got a future in elder law.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:19:01–00:19:03

### 49. get into sth.

**意思：** 进入某领域／开始从事某事

> No, I'm serious. I... I've thought about getting into elder law myself.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:19:05–00:19:10

### 50. have sb. on your side

**意思：** 有某人站在你这边／支持你

> Getting old sucks. Seniors need someone on their side.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:19:22–00:19:27

### 51. track sb. down

**意思：** 设法找到某人

> I'll see if I can, um, track Jimmy down. I'll call you back. S-sure.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:19:53–00:19:59

### 52. I need you to do sth.

**意思：** 我需要你……（要求对方行动，可能有权威口吻）

> Sir, I need you to get down from there.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:20:49–00:20:50

### 53. might as well do sth.

**意思：** 与做……差不多／不如干脆……（此处夸张地指出等同伤害）

> You might as well throw him into a microwave.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:20:57–00:20:58

### 54. I need to hear it from you.

**意思：** 我要听你本人亲口说（确认承诺／表态）

> I need you to say the words, Jimmy. I need to hear it from you.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:21:15–00:21:18

### 55. I know how it sounds, but…

**意思：** 我知道听起来很……，但……（预先回应怀疑）

> Yeah, look, I know how it sounds, but it's real.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:21:30–00:21:33

### 56. How come you didn't…?

**意思：** 你怎么没……？（口语追问原因，可能责备）

> How come you didn't call me? Uh, why did I have to hear about this through my brother's prick of a law partner?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:22:07–00:22:13

### 57. I'd rather not.

**意思：** 我还是不想／还是不了（委婉拒绝；rather 后接动词原形）

> I'd rather not.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:22:34–00:22:36

### 58. for reasons unknown

**意思：** 由于不明原因（较正式）

> For reasons unknown, my nervous system has become sensitized to certain frequencies of electromagnetic radiation.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:23:40–00:23:47

### 59. live with sth.

**意思：** 承受、长期忍受某种不适／处境

> That's a... that's a long time to live with discomfort.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:24:38–00:24:41

### 60. I never said that.

**意思：** 我可没那样说（否认对方扣上的判断）

> Oh, there it is. You think I'm crazy. I never said that.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:24:41–00:24:49

### 61. Otherwise, you wouldn't…

**意思：** 否则你就不会……（用对方行为支持推断）

> No, you didn't, because you're very polite, but you think it. Otherwise, you wouldn't be talking about commitment.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:24:49–00:24:55

### 62. Who the hell wouldn't?

**意思：** 谁会不……啊？（粗口反问，表示这很正常）

> You find that idea distressing. Who the hell wouldn't?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:24:55–00:24:58

### 63. Wouldn't you agree?

**意思：** 你不也认同吗？（寻求支持，也可能施压）

> Anyone who's spent more than a few minutes with me knows that this isn't some kind of delusion. Wouldn't you agree, Kim?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:25:07–00:25:13

### 64. the better part of sth.

**意思：** 某段时间的大部分／近乎整段时间

> You've known me for the better part of a decade.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:25:17–00:25:19

### 65. If I thought for one second…, I'd…

**意思：** 只要我觉得……有一丝可能，我就会……（强调愿意，现实中不相信）

> If I thought for one second you could cure me, believe me, I'd jump at the chance.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:25:25–00:25:30

### 66. jump at the chance

**意思：** 欣然抓住机会、求之不得

> If I thought for one second you could cure me, believe me, I'd jump at the chance.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:25:25–00:25:30

### 67. with all due respect

**意思：** 恕我直言／尽管尊重你（引出异议，可能不客气）

> But with all due respect, psychiatry doesn't have the answers.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:25:30–00:25:33

### 68. subject sb. to sth.

**意思：** 使某人遭受某种待遇／痛苦

> Do you think subjecting me to this kind of pain for a month is the least drastic means?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:25:46–00:25:51

### 69. May I ask you a question…?

**意思：** 我能问你一个问题吗？（礼貌转入提问）

> May I ask you a question, just for my own interest? How do you meet the needs of daily life? What do you do for light, or... How do you prepare food?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:26:07–00:26:16

### 70. meet the needs of sth.

**意思：** 满足某方面的需要

> How do you meet the needs of daily life?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:26:10–00:26:13

### 71. make do

**意思：** 将就着应付／用现有条件凑合

> It's not ideal, but I make do.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:26:24–00:26:28

### 72. Would you mind if I did sth.?

**意思：** 你介意我……吗？（请求自己做某事的许可）

> Would you mind if I took a moment to speak with your brother?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:26:31–00:26:35

### 73. a dirty trick

**意思：** 卑鄙的招数／不光彩的伎俩

> That was a dirty trick. That wasn't a trick... It was a demonstration.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:26:56–00:27:00

### 74. you and me put together

**意思：** 你我加起来（比较两人合计的能力等）

> I'm not saying it's 100% real, okay? But Chuck is smarter than you and me put together, so we're not talking him out of this, and I'm not sending him to a rubber room.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:27:05–00:27:13

### 75. talk sb. out of sth.

**意思：** 说服某人放弃某个想法／行动

> But Chuck is smarter than you and me put together, so we're not talking him out of this, and I'm not sending him to a rubber room.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:27:06–00:27:13

### 76. do the heavy lifting

**意思：** 承担最辛苦、最主要的工作

> So you're ready to give up. Who's giving up, huh? I'm... I'm the one doing the heavy lifting here.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:27:21–00:27:27

### 77. be in sb.'s power

**意思：** 在某人的能力／权限范围内

> If you testify in a custodial hearing, he can get the help he needs... That's in your power.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:27:38–00:27:42

### 78. be looking at sth.

**意思：** 面临某种结果；此处可能面临长期收治

> I mean, he could burn his house down or the entire neighborhood, and then you're looking at a commitment of 10 to 20 years.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:27:51–00:27:57

### 79. Doing sth. won't make it go away.

**意思：** 光是……不会让问题消失（指出回避无效）

> Ignoring this won't make it go away.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:28:13–00:28:15

### 80. All I know is…

**意思：** 我只知道……（把判断限定到确定的部分）

> All I know is... He needs help.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:28:24–00:28:27

### 81. sign off on sth.

**意思：** 正式认可／签字批准某事

> I want you to know that I've talked to the D.A., and he absolutely will not sign off on any commitment papers.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:29:14–00:29:20

### 82. be of sound mind

**意思：** 心智健全（法律、医疗语境）

> This is a physical condition, not a mental one. Chuck is of sound mind. I think we can all agree on that.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:29:20–00:29:26

### 83. I think we can all agree (that)…

**意思：** 我想大家都能认同……（寻求共同立场）

> I think we can all agree on that.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:29:23–00:29:26

### 84. You're a real peach.

**意思：** 你可真是个好人啊（此处讽刺，字面可赞人）

> You're a real peach, Howard. Of course you don't want Chuck committed. No, I don't. Why would I?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:29:27–00:29:33

### 85. hash sth. out

**意思：** 详细讨论、把问题说清楚／解决掉

> Jesus, Jimmy. Now? You want to hash that out while he's lying in here?

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:29:38–00:29:41

### 86. stay on point

**意思：** 紧扣当前话题／别跑题

> Yes. Let's stay on point. We're talking about the well-being of a man whom we all love very much.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:29:46–00:29:52

### 87. be at play

**意思：** 起作用、影响局面

> There's many factors at play. Spite doesn't have to be one of them.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:29:52–00:29:55

### 88. be on the fence

**意思：** 犹豫不决，尚未选定立场

> I was on the fence till you showed up, but thanks for helping me decide.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:29:57–00:29:59

### 89. a cash cow

**意思：** 稳定赚钱的人／业务、摇钱树

> Wave bye-bye to your cash cow, 'cause it's leaving the pasture.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:29:59–00:30:04

### 90. see sb. sweat

**意思：** 看某人紧张、坐立不安（此处想看对方难受，非字面流汗）

> I just want to see that son of a bitch sweat. It's so obvious what he's afraid of.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:30:23–00:30:28

### 91. sth. it is.

**意思：** 那就……吧（确认已经选定的选项）

> Coffee. Coffee it is.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:31:04–00:31:06

### 92. There's nothing to talk about.

**意思：** 没什么好谈的（拒绝展开话题）

> You want to talk about this? - There's nothing to talk about. - You took the paper.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:32:00–00:32:06

### 93. What's your point?

**意思：** 你到底想说明什么？（催对方点明用意，可能不耐烦）

> What's your point? Chuck, I think you got sick 'cause you saw this story.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:32:10–00:32:17

### 94. in any event

**意思：** 无论如何／不管怎样（回到结论）

> But in any event... It's legal.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:32:55–00:32:59

### 95. That's your business.

**意思：** 那是你自己的事（界定责任或不干涉）

> If you want to advertise, that's your business.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:32:59–00:33:01

### 96. sth. being the operative word

**意思：** 重点就在……这个词（强调关键字）

> "Business" being the operative word there, Chuck, because I-I have gotten business off of this... Legit business... wills, uh, trusts.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:33:01–00:33:10

### 97. a one-time thing

**意思：** 一次性的事、仅此一次

> The billboard was a one-time thing.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:33:44–00:33:46

### 98. get the ball rolling

**意思：** 把事情启动起来／使事情开始推进

> I just needed some razzmatazz, you know, to get the ball rolling, some showmanship... That's all it was.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:33:48–00:33:53

### 99. from here on out

**意思：** 从现在起、今后（美式口语）

> From here on out, I'm gonna play by the rules. As any lawyer should.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:33:55–00:33:59

### 100. play by the rules

**意思：** 遵守规则行事

> From here on out, I'm gonna play by the rules. As any lawyer should.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:33:55–00:33:59

### 101. be on the up-and-up

**意思：** 诚实合法、正当可靠（口语）

> I... I'm on the up-and-up, okay? I will be good.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:34:06–00:34:11

### 102. dead and buried

**意思：** 早已结束、彻底过去（原例指旧身份／旧行为）

> Slippin' Jimmy... He's back in Cicero, dead and buried.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:34:11–00:34:16

### 103. We'll see.

**意思：** 以后看吧／走着瞧（暂不认同或不作承诺）

> We'll see.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:34:23–00:34:25

### 104. the truth will come out

**意思：** 真相最终会显露出来

> I'm sure the truth will come out, if, indeed, that's what we're all interested in.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:34:38–00:34:43

### 105. on the first try

**意思：** 第一次尝试就……

> Most people get it on the first try.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:37:59–00:38:01

### 106. You'll be pleased to know…

**意思：** 你会很高兴得知……（原例调侃地报告情况）

> Well, you'll be pleased to know I have the requisite stickers.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:38:01–00:38:06

### 107. Be still my heart.

**意思：** 哎呀，真让我激动（此处反讽：毫无惊喜）

> Well, be still my heart.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:38:06–00:38:09

### 108. I couldn't… if I tried.

**意思：** 就算想……也做不到（原例字面是“想过个糟糕夜晚都难”；是否反讽待音视频核）

> Good night. Couldn't have a bad one if I tried. - Good night. - Night.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:38:27–00:39:07

### 109. You and me both.

**意思：** 我也一样（表示两人处境／感受相同）

> Long way from home, aren't you? You and me both.

**来源：** S01E05 · 00:43:18–00:43:22


## S01E06

### 1. You asked for it.

**意思：** 这可是你要求的（也可警告：后果自负）

> Higher? Okay, you asked for it.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:04:01–00:04:04

### 2. Don't “sth.” me.

**意思：** 别对我来这一套……（复述对方刚说的话来制止）

> Don't "aw" me.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:04:11–00:04:13

### 3. How do you like sth.?

**意思：** 你觉得……怎么样？（问体验／看法）

> So, how do you like it... Out west? I like it. Wide-open spaces.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:04:29–00:04:37

### 4. settle in

**意思：** 逐渐适应新地方／新生活

> And Kaylee? Yeah, she's settling in.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:04:53–00:04:59

### 5. for the duration

**意思：** 一直待到某段时期结束；原文补充 indefinitely，表示长期留下

> How long you in town for, Mike? I'm here... For the duration, indefinitely.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:05:12–00:05:18

### 6. I'm sorry it took me so long.

**意思：** 抱歉，我拖了这么久（迟来的行动／恢复）

> And I'm sorry it took me so long. But I'm not like I was. I'm back. I'm solid. I want to be here for you, for Kaylee, for my family.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:05:23–00:05:34

### 7. be here for sb.

**意思：** 在这里支持、陪伴某人

> I want to be here for you, for Kaylee, for my family.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:05:29–00:05:34

### 8. move on from sth.

**意思：** 走出某事，继续生活

> Mike, I got to ask... I mean... I know I just need to move on from this, but I keep thinking about something.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:05:45–00:05:56

### 9. Different how?

**意思：** 具体哪里不同？（追问对方模糊的描述）

> Different how? Different... I don't know.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:06:04–00:06:09

### 10. snap at sb.

**意思：** 对某人突然发火、厉声说话

> He snapped at me about the stupidest things.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:06:15–00:06:18

### 11. sth. is up

**意思：** 出了什么事／有情况（常暗示不对劲）

> I started to think something's up at work or... or someone's sick, something like that, but he wouldn't talk to me. He clammed right up.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:06:18–00:06:25

### 12. clam up

**意思：** 闭口不谈、突然不肯说话

> but he wouldn't talk to me. He clammed right up.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:06:22–00:06:25

### 13. As far as I remember,…

**意思：** 就我记得的而言……（限定记忆可靠范围）

> I don't... as far as I remember, he seemed okay. He seemed okay to me.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:06:46–00:06:51

### 14. make sth. out

**意思：** 听清、辨认出某内容（此处没听清谈话）

> I couldn't make it out. It was something about... I don't know. I couldn't hear it.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:07:35–00:07:41

### 15. call sb. out

**意思：** 质问、当面指出某人的行为／问题

> But the next morning, I called him out... "What the hell was that about? What's going on?" And he wouldn't talk to me.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:07:43–00:07:50

### 16. cat around

**意思：** 四处寻欢、乱搞男女关系（俚语；此处怀疑丈夫出轨）

> For one hot second, I thought maybe he was catting around... No, he wouldn't do that.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:08:02–00:08:08

### 17. be thick as thieves

**意思：** 亲密无间、关系非常好（不指真是盗贼）

> You were thick as thieves, the two of you.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:08:14–00:08:16

### 18. It makes me think (that)…

**意思：** 这让我觉得／推测……（指出推断依据）

> And there was something in his voice. It was... How he was talking. It makes me think it was you.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:08:16–00:08:24

### 19. a heart-to-heart with sb.

**意思：** 与某人推心置腹的谈话

> Stace, I don't think it was. I can't recall any late-night heart-to-hearts with him, not around then. Maybe it was a C.I. or a case.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:08:24–00:08:35

### 20. over and over

**意思：** 一遍又一遍、反复

> I know what you're doing... replaying it over and over, thinking, "if I'd noticed this or changed that", maybe I could have done something."

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:08:37–00:08:45

### 21. If I'd…, I could have…

**意思：** 要是当时我……，就可能本来可以……（回想未实现的可能）

> I know what you're doing... replaying it over and over, thinking, "if I'd noticed this or changed that", maybe I could have done something."

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:08:37–00:08:45

### 22. You don't think I haven't…?

**意思：** 你不会以为我没……过吧？（双重否定反驳对方的猜想）

> You don't think I haven't had those thoughts? I do. Every day.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:08:45–00:08:53

### 23. beat yourself up

**意思：** 自责、反复责怪自己

> You got to quit beating yourself up like this.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:08:53–00:08:56

### 24. That's all there is to it.

**意思：** 事情就是这样／就这么回事（收束解释）

> Matty's gone. That's really all there is to it.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:08:59–00:09:05

### 25. I guess that's that.

**意思：** 看来也就这样了／只能到此为止（接受终局）

> I guess that's that.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:09:14–00:09:16

### 26. It's getting on.

**意思：** 时间不早了（委婉结束谈话）

> It's getting on. I got to... Get Kaylee her dinner and put her to bed.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:09:20–00:09:28

### 27. drop by

**意思：** 顺道过来、短暂拜访

> Well, if you want me to drop by and keep an eye on her, I... I want to help.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:09:35–00:09:42

### 28. keep an eye on sb./sth.

**意思：** 照看、留意某人／物（此处看孩子）

> Well, if you want me to drop by and keep an eye on her, I... I want to help.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:09:35–00:09:42

### 29. That ought to do it.

**意思：** 这样应该就可以了（预期足以完成／解决）

> Bet there's a story there. - Isn't there always? - Yeah. All right. Little sting here. Okay. That ought to do it.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:10:46–00:10:58

### 30. You're gonna want to do sth.

**意思：** 你最好／接下来应该……（口语建议，并非预测愿望）

> You're gonna want to take it easy. Keep it clean. Keep it dry. Let it heal.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:11:00–00:11:07

### 31. take it easy

**意思：** 放轻松、别劳累（此处让伤口恢复）

> You're gonna want to take it easy. Keep it clean. Keep it dry. Let it heal.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:11:00–00:11:07

### 32. throw sth. in

**意思：** 额外附送／搭上某物

> I can, uh, throw some pills in for the pain. This stuff's essentially Vicodin. I can give it to you for $25 a pill... maybe drop the price to $15 for something a little less human-centric.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:11:25–00:11:39
