### 33. on the house

**意思：** 由店家免费招待／赠送

> Tell you what... take a couple on the house. In case you change your mind.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:11:43–00:11:49

### 34. in case sb. does sth.

**意思：** 以防／万一某人……（预先准备）

> In case you change your mind.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:11:47–00:11:49

### 35. pass through

**意思：** 路过、暂时经过某地

> Passing through or sticking around?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:11:55–00:11:57

### 36. stick around

**意思：** 留下来待一阵、暂不离开

> Passing through or sticking around?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:11:55–00:11:57

### 37. ain't nothing but a thing

**意思：** 没什么大不了的（俚语，原例省略主语）

> It's just a couple of questions. Uh, ain't nothing but a thing.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:12:34–00:12:39

### 38. I expected more… from you.

**意思：** 我原以为你会更……（表达失望／施压）

> I got to say, I, uh, expected more cooperation from you on this, cop to cop.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:12:46–00:12:51

### 39. You want to tell me…?

**意思：** 你能告诉我……吗？（口语追问，可带责问）

> You want to tell me what we're doing here?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:13:52–00:13:54

### 40. Here's what's gonna happen.

**意思：** 接下来这样办（宣布流程／安排）

> All right, here's what's gonna happen.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:14:11–00:14:13

### 41. Why, pray tell, would I do that?

**意思：** 请问，我凭什么那么做？（故作礼貌的反问）

> - And why, pray tell, would I do that? - Because I'm asking you to.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:14:33–00:14:36

### 42. I doubt (that)…

**意思：** 我怀疑是否……／恐怕不……（表达不相信）

> I am asking you to take a few ounces of lukewarm coffee and spill it on him. I doubt that satisfies the definition of "assault," but, hey, you're the lawyer.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:14:40–00:14:53

### 43. How silly of me.

**意思：** 我可真傻啊（此处自我挖苦以讽刺对方）

> Right. How silly of me.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:14:53–00:14:55

### 44. aid and abet

**意思：** 协助、教唆（通常指违法；较正式）

> All you want is for me to aid and abet you ripping off that guy's notepad, 'cause that's what you're doing here, right?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:14:55–00:15:00

### 45. You can't be serious.

**意思：** 你不是认真的吧／这也太离谱了（难以相信）

> Huh? What... Are you nuts? You can't be serious.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:15:00–00:15:04

### 46. honest-to-God

**意思：** 真正的、货真价实的（加强语气，口语）

> I'm gonna behave like an honest-to-God, law-abiding, licensed attorney, 'cause, clearly, you need one with whatever the hell is going on here.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:15:14–00:15:23

### 47. dot your i's and cross your t's

**意思：** 一丝不苟，检查所有细节

> Now, those two jokers out in the hallway... I'm gonna make sure they dot their I's and cross their t's... everything square and above board.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:15:23–00:15:29

### 48. above board

**意思：** 公开、诚实合法，不搞小动作

> I'm gonna make sure they dot their I's and cross their t's... everything square and above board.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:15:25–00:15:29

### 49. Not gonna happen.

**意思：** 绝不可能／我不会答应（断然拒绝）

> But this little Juan Valdez bump-and-dump... no. Not gonna happen.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:15:34–00:15:39

### 50. sit in with sb.

**意思：** 参与旁听／陪同在场（此处陪同审讯）

> Detective Escalara will be sitting in with us, as per APD policy. Okay with you, right? Fine with me.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:15:46–00:15:54

### 51. as per sth.

**意思：** 按照某项规定／约定（较正式）

> Detective Escalara will be sitting in with us, as per APD policy. Okay with you, right? Fine with me.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:15:46–00:15:54

### 52. fill sb. in

**意思：** 把详情告诉某人、补齐背景

> "Hoffman." "Fensky." Fill me in. Assume I know absolutely nothing about my client here, all right?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:16:07–00:16:16

### 53. Don't let sth. fool you.

**意思：** 别被……骗了／别让表象误导你

> Look, don't let Mr. Ehrmantraut's dancing eyes and bubbly, bon vivant personality fool you. He's actually... believe it or not... somewhat taciturn.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:16:18–00:16:28

### 54. believe it or not

**意思：** 信不信由你（引出可能让人意外的事实）

> He's actually... believe it or not... somewhat taciturn.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:16:24–00:16:28

### 55. As you probably gathered,…

**意思：** 想必你已经看出来／明白了……

> Okay, Mr. McGill, as you probably gathered, we, Detective Sanders and I... work for the Philadelphia Police Department, as did Mr. Ehrmantraut for nearly 30 years.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:16:36–00:16:44

### 56. on the job

**意思：** 在岗位上、从事该工作（此处已有两年任职经历）

> He was a rookie officer with about two years on the job. - He was a good cop. - Yes, he was.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:16:51–00:16:56

### 57. get out of hand

**意思：** 失去控制、局面无法收拾

> Unfortunately, things got out of hand. The three of them were ambushed, and, uh, Matt didn't make it out.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:17:08–00:17:14

### 58. make it out

**意思：** 逃出来、成功离开（此处没有活着逃出）

> The three of them were ambushed, and, uh, Matt didn't make it out. Hoffman and Fensky returned fire, but the shooter got away.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:17:10–00:17:17

### 59. come up short

**意思：** 没取得所需结果；此处没找到破案线索

> We chased a few leads, shook up the usual suspects... came up short.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:17:17–00:17:22

### 60. beat the bushes

**意思：** 四处搜寻、竭力寻找（别与 beat around the bush 绕弯子混淆）

> Anyways, we kept beating the bushes, kept coming up dry until three months ago.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:17:26–00:17:31

### 61. come up dry

**意思：** 查无结果、空手而归（寻找线索语境）

> Anyways, we kept beating the bushes, kept coming up dry until three months ago.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:17:26–00:17:31

### 62. turn up dead

**意思：** 被发现已经死亡

> That's when Hoffman and Fensky both turned up dead in a vacant lot out in nicetown... Again, an ambush of some kind, it looks like.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:17:31–00:17:38

### 63. be mixed up in sth.

**意思：** 卷入某种不正当／麻烦事

> And with whoever killed Matt out there in the wind, we're thinking... Well, it's our operating theory that Hoffman and Fensky were mixed up in something, some bad business, and maybe that got Matt killed.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:17:38–00:17:49

### 64. a Hail Mary

**意思：** 孤注一掷、成功希望渺茫的最后一招（美式比喻）

> Uh, Mike, this is a hail Mary. Uh, beyond that. I mean, if we had any hard evidence or leads, we wouldn't be out here.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:17:54–00:18:02

### 65. hard evidence

**意思：** 确凿证据（hard 不是物理硬度）

> I mean, if we had any hard evidence or leads, we wouldn't be out here.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:17:59–00:18:02

### 66. in particular

**意思：** 尤其、具体某一个；原例问是否与特定某人同行

> Yeah, but did you see them with anyone in particular?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:18:32–00:18:33

### 67. I couldn't tell you.

**意思：** 我说不上来／无法告诉你（此处记不清）

> I couldn't tell you. I was... You know how I was. How you doing these days? Feeling like I crawled out from the bottom of a bottle and working hard to stay there.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:18:33–00:18:45

### 68. dredge up the past

**意思：** 翻出旧事、揭旧伤疤（常带负面意味）

> Although I've got to say... doesn't help much, dredging up the past like this.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:18:45–00:18:51

### 69. It couldn't have been…

**意思：** 当时不可能是……（对过去作否定推断）

> uh, when did you come out here to Albuquerque? It couldn't have been, uh, too much later. I think it was the very next day.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:18:52–00:19:00

### 70. You didn't think to do sth.?

**意思：** 你没想到要……吗？（事后质问遗漏的行动）

> You didn't think to, uh, stick around once you heard the news? I don't think I heard the news till I was west of Kansas City.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:19:04–00:19:11

### 71. take a flier on sb./sth.

**意思：** 冒险试一试某人／某事；此处抱着一线希望来询问

> Sorry. I got nothing. They weren't my people. I'm sorry you guys took a flier on me. I wish it'd have paid off.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:19:23–00:19:30

### 72. pay off

**意思：** 得到回报、取得预期成果

> I'm sorry you guys took a flier on me. I wish it'd have paid off.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:19:27–00:19:30

### 73. kick around

**意思：** 在某地闲待／逗留（原例警察再留几天）

> Listen, we're probably gonna kick around for another couple of days, in case you think of something, all right?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:19:39–00:19:43

### 74. What's that supposed to mean?

**意思：** 那是什么意思？（往往带质疑、不满）

> What the hell's that? What's that supposed to mean, huh?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:20:55–00:21:01

### 75. In case you missed it,…

**意思：** 提醒你一下……（对方可能没注意到；可带讽刺）

> But in case you missed it, your friends from Philly back there... they think you killed two cops.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:21:05–00:21:11

### 76. How could you possibly think that?

**意思：** 你怎么可能那么想？（强烈难以置信／质问）

> How could you possibly think that? That's your husband, the father of your child. I didn't say that. I s... I told them I found money.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:22:53–00:23:00

### 77. tuck sth. away

**意思：** 把某物藏好、存放在不显眼处

> Matt must have tucked it away in there, 'cause this was cash... $5,000, $6,000 from God knows where.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:23:04–00:23:12

### 78. God knows where

**意思：** 天知道哪里／不知道从哪里（强调来源不明）

> $5,000, $6,000 from God knows where.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:23:08–00:23:12

### 79. live paycheck to paycheck

**意思：** 靠每次发薪勉强过活、没余钱；原文省略 live

> $5,000, $6,000 from God knows where. I mean, we were basically paycheck to paycheck. Where the hell did he get it?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:23:08–00:23:15

### 80. come to sb.

**意思：** 找某人求助／商量（此处问为何没有来找自己）

> Why didn't you ask me? Why didn't you come to me? I couldn't. I knew what it would do to you.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:23:15–00:23:22

### 81. All I want is for sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 我只要某人……（集中说明诉求，for 引出动作的人）

> All I want is for whoever killed Matty to rot in a cell for the rest of their life, and then I want whatever's left of them just dumped in the trash... that's what I want.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:23:40–00:23:48

### 82. What difference would it make if…?

**意思：** 即使……又有什么区别？（强调不影响立场）

> I don't care where it leads, what it uncovers. I mean, what difference would it make if he was... Anything? I'd still love him. I'd still miss him. He'd still be gone.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:23:48–00:23:59

### 83. be straight with sb.

**意思：** 对某人坦诚、直接讲真话

> Well, so be straight with me! Right now. This is it, Mike. What was that phone call before he died? Don't bullshit me.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:24:01–00:24:09

### 84. That's between sb. and sb.

**意思：** 那是两个人之间的私事（拒绝向他人说明）

> That was between me and my son. So you're admitting it was you.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:24:09–00:24:15

### 85. Get sth. through your head.

**意思：** 搞清楚／牢牢记住某事（强硬、不耐烦的命令）

> He wasn't dirty! God damn you! You get that through your head! My son wasn't dirty!

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:24:15–00:24:24

### 86. cry it all out

**意思：** 尽情哭出来、把悲痛哭出来

> Just let him cry it all out, okay?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:27:11–00:27:14

### 87. It's been a minute.

**意思：** 好久不见／有一阵子了（口语，非一分钟）

> Good to see you, too. It's been a minute.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:27:22–00:27:25

### 88. give sb. a lift

**意思：** 开车送某人一程

> Hey, how about I give you a lift home?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:28:06–00:28:07

### 89. in my book

**意思：** 在我看来、按我的标准

> That's a big minus in my book.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:28:39–00:28:41

### 90. be on the lookout

**意思：** 留心、警惕可能出现的事物

> Well, I will be on the lookout.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:28:41–00:28:47

### 91. in no time

**意思：** 很快、马上

> Okay. Easy. We're gonna have you home in no time.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:30:04–00:30:09

### 92. tie one on

**意思：** 痛饮、喝醉（俚语）

> You really tied one on good back there, buddy.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:30:47–00:30:49

### 93. put it away

**意思：** 大口喝／喝下大量酒（口语，it 指酒）

> You really tied one on good back there, buddy. Shit, yeah, he did... put it away like they were fixing to bring back prohibition.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:30:47–00:30:54

### 94. be fixing to do sth.

**意思：** 正打算／马上要……（美国地区口语）

> Shit, yeah, he did... put it away like they were fixing to bring back prohibition.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:30:49–00:30:54

### 95. talk out of your ass

**意思：** 胡说八道（粗俗）

> Hey, hey, back at McClure's, you were talking out your ass, hmm? "I know it was you." What's that supposed to mean, huh?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:30:56–00:31:03

### 96. get sth. off your chest

**意思：** 把压在心里的话／烦恼说出来

> You got something you want to get off your chest, something you want to say to us?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:31:06–00:31:11

### 97. for nothing

**意思：** 毫无意义／白白地（此处杀人没有必要）

> You killed him. You killed Matty. And you killed him for nothing. You killed him because you were scared of what you thought he might do.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:31:11–00:31:29

### 98. see sth. coming

**意思：** 预见某事会发生、料到后果

> It's too much for the old man. So Mikey here decided to eat his gun. It's tragic, but anyone could see this coming, hmm? He's drinking himself to death.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:33:22–00:33:31

### 99. do sb. a favor

**意思：** 帮某人一个忙；原例为犯罪者的自我开脱

> We're doing him a favor.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:33:31–00:33:33

### 100. That's what I would have done if I were you.

**意思：** 换作是你，我也会那样做（回评对方过去的选择）

> Smart. That's what I would have done if I were you.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:33:33–00:33:36

### 101. let sth. slide

**意思：** 睁一只眼闭一只眼、放任不管

> You let some things slide and you look the other way. You bust a drug dealer that has more cash than you'll ever earn in a lifetime. Some of it doesn't make it back into evidence... So what?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:35:16–00:35:32

### 102. look the other way

**意思：** 对不当行为装作没看见

> You let some things slide and you look the other way. You bust a drug dealer that has more cash than you'll ever earn in a lifetime. Some of it doesn't make it back into evidence... So what?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:35:16–00:35:32

### 103. turn sb. in

**意思：** 向当局检举某人／交出某人

> You turn in your buddy, you're screwing yourself.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:35:59–00:36:05

### 104. go along to get along

**意思：** 为了合群、相安无事而随大流（通常带妥协意味）

> You go along to get along. And you went along. I did.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:36:05–00:36:22

### 105. get to sb.

**意思：** 拉拢／腐蚀某人（此处使警察参与受贿，不是让人烦心）

> Fensky got to Hoffman early... kickbacks from some gang or another... protection, basically. And Hoffman went to Matty and offered to cut him in.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:36:37–00:36:49

### 106. cut sb. in

**意思：** 让某人分一份利益／加入赚钱的勾当

> And Hoffman went to Matty and offered to cut him in. Only fair, right? They were partners.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:36:46–00:36:51

### 107. turn on sb.

**意思：** 转而反对／背叛某人（此处举报）

> do the right thing, shut 'em down. Oh, my God. And you let him? That's why he got killed... 'cause he was gonna turn on those guys?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:37:05–00:37:11

### 108. get locked up

**意思：** 被关进监狱

> Prison... getting locked up with everybody you put away.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:37:27–00:37:32

### 109. put sb. away

**意思：** 把某人送进监狱（与前文喝酒的 put it away 区分）

> Prison... getting locked up with everybody you put away.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:37:27–00:37:32

### 110. talk sense

**意思：** 说有道理的话；原例是劝其采取自己认为“现实”的做法

> I talked sense. No one was getting hurt. But if you go to the I.A., if you even look like you're going...

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:37:40–00:37:52

### 111. get by

**意思：** 勉强维持、过活

> I told him I did it, too, that I was like Hoffman, getting by, and that's what you heard that night...

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:38:36–00:38:45

### 112. talk sb. down

**意思：** 劝某人冷静、打消激烈想法（不同于 E02 把刑罚谈低）

> and that's what you heard that night... me talking him down, him kicking and screaming until the fight went out of him.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:38:42–00:38:50

### 113. kicking and screaming

**意思：** 极不情愿、激烈反抗（此处从抵抗到屈服）

> me talking him down, him kicking and screaming until the fight went out of him.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:38:45–00:38:50

### 114. put sb. on a pedestal

**意思：** 过度崇拜、把某人理想化

> He put me up on a pedestal... And I had to show him, that I was down in the gutter with the rest of them. Broke my boy.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:38:50–00:39:10

### 115. be down in the gutter

**意思：** 处于道德低下／堕落的境地（原例自责）

> He put me up on a pedestal... And I had to show him, that I was down in the gutter with the rest of them. Broke my boy.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:38:50–00:39:10

### 116. get sb. to do sth.

**意思：** 使／说服某人做某事（原例强烈自责）

> I got Matty to take the money. And they killed him two days later.

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:39:35–00:39:45

### 117. sb. would never do sth., not even to do sth.

**意思：** 某人绝不会……，即便为了……也不会（强调原则／拒绝）

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:39:54–00:40:10

### 118. The question is…

**意思：** 问题在于……（把谈话收束到关键）

> You know what happened. The question is... can you live with it?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:41:12–00:41:25

### 119. Can you live with it?

**意思：** 你能接受／承受这件事吗？（此处指能否接受真相的道德后果）

> You know what happened. The question is... can you live with it?

**来源：** S01E06 · 00:41:12–00:41:25

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:01:46–00:01:54

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:25:42–00:25:48

### 93. sing sb.'s own praises

**意思：** 自夸；夸耀自己

> Much as I'd love to sing my own praises,

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:25:42–00:25:44

### 94. I doubt I could…

**意思：** 我怀疑自己能不能……／我恐怕做不到……

> I doubt I could get you a deal as good as the one she already has.

**意思：** 毫无疑问；确实

**意思：** 详细地／长时间地（此处详细讨论）

### 98. come what may

**意思：** 不管发生什么

> Come what may.

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:27:15–00:27:16

### 99. not be playing with a full deck

**意思：** 脑子不太正常／判断有问题（贬义口语）

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:29:07–00:29:10

**意思：** 对某人／某事评价很高（此处反讽）

**意思：** 拼命工作（粗俗口语）

### 105. guilty as sin

**意思：** 确实有罪／罪责明摆着（口语强调）

> he's guilty as sin.

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:30:05–00:30:06

### 106. Thanks for not doing sth.

**意思：** 谢谢你没有……（此处带玩笑的感谢）

### 107. Am I correct in assuming (that)…?

**意思：** 我这样认为没错吧？（核实自己的推断）

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:38:22–00:38:27

### 108. I love what you've done with the place.

**意思：** 我很喜欢你把这里布置／改造成这样（赞美环境）

> Oh, I love what you've done with the place.

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:38:55–00:38:58

### 109. get to the nitty-gritty

**意思：** 谈到实质细节／关键内容

### 110. By sth., you mean…?

**意思：** 你说的……是指……？（确认指代／含义）

> - By "it," you mean... - Where is it?! Oh. You mean the money.

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:40:08–00:40:14

### 111. Takes one to know one.

**意思：** 你也是这号人，才认得出来（反击指责）

> - Takes one to know one, doesn't it? - He took it?

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:40:24–00:40:26

### 112. Here's what we're gonna do.

**意思：** 接下来我们这么办（提出具体安排）

### 113. even on a good day

**意思：** 即使在情况好的日子里（暗示平常更差）

> I... I can see how upset you are, and, even on a good day, you and logic are... But think about what you just said.

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:40:39–00:40:48

### 114. bring sth. upon oneself

**意思：** 自己招致某种后果

### 115. be on the hook for sth.

**意思：** 要为某事负责／承担后果

**意思：** 往好的一面想

### 117. have nothing to lose

**意思：** 没什么可失去；豁得出去

> Thing you folks need to know about me... I got nothing to lose.

**来源：** S01E07 · 00:41:58–00:42:03

### 118. put up with sth.

**意思：** 忍受／容忍某事

### 119. I will not be treated this way.

**意思：** 我绝不接受别人这样对待我（明确拒绝）

> - I will not be treated this way. - Betsy...

## S01E08

### 1. call sth. to sb.'s attention

**意思：** 提请某人注意某事（较正式）

> I must call to your attention the newly passed Environmental Impact and Sustainability Assurance Act, a statute that, as you know, went into effect without any ex post facto provisions in place.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:01:53–00:02:03

### 2. go into effect

**意思：** 生效；开始实施

> a statute that, as you know, went into effect without any ex post facto provisions in place.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:01:58–00:02:03

### 3. in place

**意思：** 已具备／已设置好（此处法律条款）

> without any ex post facto provisions in place.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:02:00–00:02:03

### 4. It'll just take two seconds.

**意思：** 就花一小会儿（用短暂耗时减轻请求负担）

> It'll just take two seconds. I promise.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:02:06–00:02:08

### 5. I thought if… maybe I can, too.

**意思：** 我想如果别人能……，也许我也可以（解释尝试的想法）

> I... I thought if Kim could do it, maybe I can, too, you know?

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:02:26–00:02:30

### 6. third time's the charm

**意思：** 第三次就会成功／走运

> third time's the charm.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:03:08–00:03:09

### 7. This must have taken you…

**意思：** 这肯定花了你……（根据结果推断耗时）

> How... this must have taken you years. And you kept it a secret all this time.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:03:09–00:03:15

### 8. keep sth. a secret

**意思：** 把某事保密

> And you kept it a secret all this time.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:03:13–00:03:15

### 9. I don't know what to say.

**意思：** 我不知道说什么好（惊讶／感动时的回应）

> I don't know what to say.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:03:26–00:03:28

### 10. That means a lot.

**意思：** 这对我意义很大（表达感激）

> Chuck, that means a lot.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:03:36–00:03:40

### 11. take sth. up with sb.

**意思：** 就某事与某人商议／提出问题

> Well, that's a question I'm gonna have to take up with Howard and the other partners.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:03:55–00:04:00

### 12. It's not my decision alone.

**意思：** 这事不能由我一个人决定

> Uh, you know, it's not my decision alone.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:04:00–00:04:04

### 13. How could sb. say no?

**意思：** 某人怎么可能拒绝？（反问，表示应当答应）

> But how could they say no? So much drive. Jimmy, look what you've done here.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:04:04–00:04:12

### 14. on behalf of sb.

**意思：** 代表某人

> Just a little celebration on behalf of New Mexico's newest attorney.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:04:31–00:04:35

### 15. Don't mind if I do.

**意思：** 那我就不客气了（欣然接受食物等）

> Oh, here. Uh, don't mind if I do.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:04:42–00:04:44

### 16. I hate to interrupt, but…

**意思：** 不想打断各位，但……（提出插话请求）

> We're all proud of you. Hey, I hate to interrupt. But could you guys give Jimmy and me a moment?

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:04:47–00:04:52

### 17. give sb. a moment

**意思：** 给某人一点时间／让某人单独谈一会儿

> But could you guys give Jimmy and me a moment? - Sure. - Yeah, absolutely.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:04:50–00:04:54

### 18. bask in the glow

**意思：** 享受成功／赞誉带来的光彩（此处含挖苦）

> Come bask in the glow.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:06:47–00:06:50

### 19. make time

**意思：** 挤出时间

> I appreciate you making time in the middle of your busy days.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:06:55–00:06:58

### 20. bring sth. to a close

**意思：** 使某事结束（较正式）

> and brings to a close an incident Mr. Kettleman deeply regrets.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:07:09–00:07:13

### 21. make amends for sth.

**意思：** 为自己的过错作补偿／弥补

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:07:13–00:07:15

### 22. I should have done sth. years ago.

**意思：** 我早就该做某事了（后悔拖到现在）

### 23. put sth. off

**意思：** 拖延／推迟某事

**意思：** 总共是……钱

**意思：** 给某人固定生活费／零花钱，限制可支配数额

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:10:12–00:10:21

### 28. I'm afraid I'm not explaining it very well.

### 29. I don't know if you noticed…

**意思：** 不知你有没有注意到……（引出提醒）

### 30. do the trick

**意思：** 管用；达到所需效果

### 31. I can't tell.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:27:14–00:27:20

### 71. I don't mind the company.

**意思：** 有人陪着我也挺好／我不介意有人在

> I don't mind the company.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:27:24–00:27:28

### 72. You look like you could use sth.

**意思：** 看起来你需要／很想来点……（体贴地提供）

> You look like you could use a cup of coffee. Good god, yes.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:27:28–00:27:34

### 73. It gets better.

**意思：** 还有更好的事／更精彩的在后面

> Well, it gets better. What's this? It's your smoking gun.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:29:35–00:29:41

### 74. a smoking gun

**意思：** 能确凿证明罪行／问题的关键证据

> It's your smoking gun. It's a, uh... invoice for syringes. Yes. From? Morrissey Medical Supply Lincoln, Nebraska.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:29:39–00:29:49

### 75. It's not a bad idea to do sth.

**意思：** 做某事是个不错的主意（原句省略主语）

> Probably not a bad idea to pull 30.47.1 from NMSA... Uh, 1978. I don't think that's gonna play, really, but it's a good opening salvo for us.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:30:00–00:30:12

### 76. right out of the gate

**意思：** 一开始／一上来就

> Oh, man. Sweet-talking right out of the gate? You want something. Make it fast.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:30:49–00:30:54

### 77. Hit me.

**意思：** 说吧／告诉我吧（此处让对方报信息）

> All right. Hit me.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:31:03–00:31:04

### 78. It would help if I knew…

**意思：** 如果我知道……会更有帮助（委婉索取信息）

> It would help if I knew what you were looking for here. Anything, everything.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:31:25–00:31:28

### 79. the wolf is at the door

**意思：** 穷困／危机迫在眉睫（此处夸张，要求赶紧做）

> Shepardize like the wolf's at the door. Jesus, Jimmy, that's gonna be thousands of pages.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:31:34–00:31:39

### 80. Pretty please with sugar on top?

**意思：** 拜托拜托，求你啦！（撒娇式请求）

> Pretty please with sugar on top?

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:31:39–00:31:41

### 81. … sounds fun and all, but no.

**意思：** ……听起来挺有趣什么的，但不行（此处反讽拒绝）

> The accountants will find it and I'll get fired, which sounds fun and all, but no.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:32:01–00:32:05

### 82. keep sth. under sb.'s hat

**意思：** 把某事保密，别说出去

> Yeah. Keep this under your hat, but it's gonna be huge... maybe a class-action, even.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:32:36–00:32:41

### 83. know sth. chapter and verse

**意思：** 对某事内容一清二楚／能详细背出来

> Look, I know Chuck's partnership agreement chapter and verse. He can work with outside parties.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:32:48–00:32:52

### 84. not be oneself

**意思：** 状态不对／不像平常的自己

> And Chuck can handle this? Last time I saw him, he was... Last time you saw him, he wasn't himself, okay?

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:33:00–00:33:05

### 85. a drop in the bucket

**意思：** 杯水车薪；相比所需数量微不足道

> Of course, it's only a drop in the bucket.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:35:12–00:35:16

### 86. the price of admission

**意思：** 参与／进入所必须接受的条件或代价（此处比喻）

> This is the price of admission.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:36:36–00:36:39

### 87. All you've got to do is do sth.

**意思：** 你只要做某事就行（减少压力，明确有限任务）

> All you got to do is sit there and look intimidating. They're scared of you already, and they should be.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:37:22–00:37:30

### 88. a laundry list

**意思：** 一长串清单（常含事项过多的意味）

> Um, we've read your revised demand. It's, uh, quite a laundry list. Very creative.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:38:31–00:38:38

### 89. make sth. stick

**意思：** 让指控／说法成立，经得起质疑

> That's cute. Y... you know, you're not gonna make this stick.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:38:45–00:38:47

### 90. Setting aside sth.,…

**意思：** 先撇开某事不谈，……

> Setting aside evidentiary concerns... whoa, whoa. Your guys couldn't afford 10 bucks for a padlock? That's on them. Public property.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:38:47–00:38:55

### 91. As far as I understand it,…

**意思：** 据我的理解，……（限定判断依据）

> As far as I understand it, no one has claimed there's a guy with a knife telling them to hand over their lunch money.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:39:07–00:39:11

### 92. That's not the case with sb./sth.

**意思：** 某人／某事的情况并不是这样

> There are a lot of assisted-living facilities out there that may be disreputable, but that's not the case with our client, not even close.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:39:11–00:39:17

### 93. rise to the level of sth.

**意思：** 达到足以被认定为某事的程度（此处法律判断）

> It doesn't rise to the level of fraud, not even close.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:39:37–00:39:39

### 94. All told,…

**意思：** 总共／合计起来，……

> We've calculated the damages. All told, it's $46,320.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:39:39–00:39:44

### 95. 数字 and change

**意思：** ……多一点（金额的零头）

> We're gonna make your clients whole... a check for the $46,000 and change right now.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:39:48–00:39:53

### 96. on top of that

**意思：** 此外／在那之外还……

> And on top of that, we'll give you another $46k to cover your expenses.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:39:53–00:39:58

### 97. You'll need to do better than that.

**意思：** 你得拿出更好的条件／表现（此处还价）

> I think you're gonna need to do better than that.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:40:13–00:40:17

### 98. I don't know if you know this, but…

**意思：** 我不知道你是否知道，不过……（引出说明；此处略居高临下）

> I... I don't know if you know this, but that's a pretty standard item in an assisted-living facility.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:40:24–00:40:29

### 99. It's not what… that matters. It's where…

**意思：** 关键不是……是什么，而是……来自哪里（对比重心）

> Correct. But it's not what it is that matters. It's where it's from. Lincoln, Nebraska.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:40:29–00:40:35

### 100. pick up what sb. is putting down

**意思：** 明白某人的意思／领会暗示（随意口语）

> Well, since you're not picking up what I'm putting down, I'll explain it to you.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:40:39–00:40:45

### 101. no less

**意思：** 而且居然还是……／竟然（强调额外事实）

> So your client accepted shipments across state lines for its enterprise. I... in the U.S. mail, no less.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:40:53–00:41:03

### 102. You know as well as I do (that)…

**意思：** 你和我一样清楚……（强调对方不该否认）

> Well, you know as well as I do RICO's used mostly for business beefs.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:41:08–00:41:12

### 103. kick in

**意思：** 开始生效／起作用

> Sedima establishes a pretty low threshold for RICO provisions to kick in.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:41:12–00:41:17

### 104. have sth. in mind

**意思：** 心里考虑着某事；有某个具体打算

> What number exactly did you have in mind? $20 million.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:41:57–00:42:02

### 105. They can't possibly…

**意思：** 他们绝不可能……（强烈排除一种可能）

> This is a big company. 12 separate facilities. They can't possibly all be in New Mexico. This is potentially a multi-state

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:42:29–00:42:38

### 106. go straight to hell

**意思：** 下地狱去／不得好死（愤怒诅咒）

> Those people can go straight to hell, as far as I'm concerned.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:43:55–00:43:59

### 107. Depends on sth.

**意思：** 要看……／取决于……（回答前先限定条件）

> Depends on the type of work you're looking for... your dos, your don'ts, your wills, your won'ts. Well, if you tell me what you've got, I'll tell you what I'll do.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:44:08–00:44:19

### 108. dos and don'ts

**意思：** 该做与不该做的事／行为规则（原句并列列举，随后戏仿扩展）

> Depends on the type of work you're looking for... your dos, your don'ts, your wills, your won'ts.

**来源：** S01E08 · 00:44:08–00:44:14


## S01E09

### 1. kick off your shoes

**意思：** 甩掉／踢掉鞋子

> Kick off your shoes like this.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:00:41–00:00:43

### 2. When was the last time you did sth.?

**意思：** 你上次做……是什么时候？（提醒久未做过）

> When was the last time you did that?

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:00:54–00:00:56

### 3. Maybe it's time sb. did sth.

**意思：** 也许是该……的时候了（委婉提议；原句口语用 go）

> Well, I think maybe... Maybe it's time we go in.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:01:14–00:01:18

### 4. have the facts on your side

**意思：** 事实对你有利

> Facts on your side, better.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:01:37–00:01:40

### 5. know what you're walking into

**意思：** 先弄清自己将面对什么局面

> Know what you're walking into.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:01:40–00:01:43

### 6. think twice about doing sth.

**意思：** 做……前多想想／有所顾忌

> Bad guys think twice about breaking into a house with a dog.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:02:40–00:02:44

### 7. break into a house

**意思：** 闯入住宅（通常指入室盗窃）

> Bad guys think twice about breaking into a house with a dog.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:02:40–00:02:44

### 8. You can always do sth.

**意思：** 实在不行你还可以……（提供备选）

> You know, you can always keep her over at my place.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:02:51–00:02:54

### 9. on what grounds?

**意思：** 依据什么理由？

> Mr. Schweikart, you're denying him access on what grounds?

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:04:41–00:04:45

### 10. stretch sth. to its limit

**意思：** 把……拉到极限；此处指强行扩大定义

> Wow, you are stretching that definition to its limit.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:05:18–00:05:21

### 11. be on fire

**意思：** 状态正旺／表现特别出色（口语）

> This mo-fo is on fire. Your baby bro kicked some Schweikart ass today.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:05:36–00:05:43

### 12. call sth. into question

**意思：** 对……提出质疑

> Documents calling into question the mental health of our elderly clients.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:05:58–00:06:03

### 13. on the grounds (that)…

**意思：** 以……为理由（较正式）

> Attempting to stop the class from being certified... on the grounds they're not competent.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:06:04–00:06:08

### 14. be drowning in paperwork

**意思：** 被大量文书工作淹没

> They're gonna continue this document dump until we're drowning in paperwork.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:06:11–00:06:15

### 15. hit sth. head on

**意思：** 正面应对……

> - It's no big deal. Just hit it head on. - Jimmy.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:06:25–00:06:28

### 16. I know you're not gonna like what I have to say, but…

**意思：** 我知道你不爱听，但……（铺垫难听的建议）

> I know you're not gonna like what I have to say next. But facts are facts.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:06:47–00:06:51

### 17. Facts are facts.

**意思：** 事实就是事实（强调无法回避）

> But facts are facts.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:06:50–00:06:51

### 18. for the good of sb.

**意思：** 为了……的利益／好处

> - No. - And for the good of the clients... - we need to refer this case... - Don't say it. Refer it out to HHM.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:06:58–00:07:04

### 19. do sth. on your own

**意思：** 独立完成……

> We can do this. We're doing great on our own.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:07:08–00:07:13

### 20. bring in heavy hitters

**意思：** 请来重量级人物／强手

> She and Ed Masry brought in heavy hitters...

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:07:23–00:07:25

### 21. get sth. across the goal line

**意思：** 把……推进到成功完成（体育比喻）

> She and Ed Masry brought in heavy hitters... from Los Angeles to get that case across the goal line.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:07:23–00:07:28

### 22. There's no shame in doing sth.

**意思：** 做……没什么丢人的（安慰／劝说）

> And that's what we need to do. And there's no shame in that.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:07:28–00:07:32

### 23. take forever

**意思：** 花非常久／没完没了（夸张口语）

> But it would take forever.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:07:44–00:07:46

### 24. Just how long do you think…?

**意思：** 你觉得到底还有多久……？（强调追问）

> Just how long do you think the residents of Sandpiper Crossing have?

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:07:46–00:07:50

### 25. carry sth. forward

**意思：** 继续推进……

> And we don't have the capital to carry it forward by ourselves.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:08:14–00:08:17

### 26. come to see (that)…

**意思：** 逐渐认识到／明白……

> I know you'll come to see that it's for the best.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:08:48–00:08:51

### 27. be for the best

**意思：** 是最合适的结果／这样最好

> I know you'll come to see that it's for the best.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:08:48–00:08:51

### 28. be as green as they come

**意思：** 再生嫩不过／毫无经验（green 指没经验）

> Guy is as green as they come, but we'll be in there to make sure things fly right.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:12:17–00:12:22

### 29. the best of the best

**意思：** 最顶尖的一批

> And it makes sense. The vet recommends the best of the best.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:12:29–00:12:32

### 30. so be it

**意思：** 那就这样吧（接受／不再争辩）

> You don't want to tell me what you're carrying, so be it.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:13:11–00:13:14

### 31. You don't have to be… about it.

**意思：** 没必要为这事表现得……（批评态度）

> But you don't have to be a douche about it.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:13:14–00:13:16

### 32. Is that what you're saying?

**意思：** 你是在这么说吗？（确认含义／质问）

> So you're saying you don't have a gun? Is that what you're saying?

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:13:21–00:13:26

### 33. have sth. on you

**意思：** 身上带着……

> I got at least two guns on me that I'll tell you about.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:13:35–00:13:37

### 34. go old school

**意思：** 采用老派做法／偏爱老派款式

> I go old school with a Wilson Combat 1911.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:13:37–00:13:40

### 35. I'd prefer something other than…

**意思：** 我更希望是……以外的东西

> If I need to talk to you, I'd prefer something other than "Hey, you."

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:14:27–00:14:31

### 36. come highly recommended

**意思：** 受到大力推荐

> Either way, you all come highly recommended so thank you.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:14:32–00:14:35

### 37. First order of business,…

**意思：** 先处理的第一件事是……

> First order of business, we have a long drive ahead of us.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:14:35–00:14:38

### 38. have a long drive ahead of you

**意思：** 接下来还得开很久的车

> First order of business, we have a long drive ahead of us.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:14:35–00:14:38

### 39. Just to be clear,…

**意思：** 先说清楚……（避免误解）

> Just to be clear that the agreed upon fee of $500 per man is... agreed upon.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:14:53–00:15:00

### 40. How about you do sth.?

**意思：** 你……怎么样？（提议对方采取行动）

> How about you give me and Man Mountain 750 each?

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:15:05–00:15:08

### 41. I didn't think I'd need sth.

**意思：** 我原以为用不着……（解释先前判断）

> - Is that true? You didn't bring a gun? - I didn't think I'd need one.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:15:14–00:15:19

### 42. It's basic common sense that…

**意思：** ……是最基本的常识

> It's basic common sense that you need a gun to protect your employer.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:15:22–00:15:25

### 43. be dead weight

**意思：** 是累赘／派不上用场的人

> You're dead weight.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:15:25–00:15:27

### 44. be a third wheel

**意思：** 是多余的那个人（此处非情侣场景）

> - He's just a third wheel without a piece. - I'll tell you what.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:15:27–00:15:30

### 45. How do you picture that happening exactly?

**意思：** 你具体想象那会怎样发生？（质疑可行性）

> Really? One of my guns? How do you picture that happening exactly?

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:15:32–00:15:36

### 46. I'll make it easy for you.

**意思：** 我给你省点事／让你更容易做到（此处挑衅）

> Here. I'll make it easy for you.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:15:53–00:15:57

### 47. I'll bet sb. would…, wouldn't they?

**意思：** 我猜你会……，对吧？（带把握的推测求证）

> I'll bet you'd have an ankle holster, wouldn't you?

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:16:28–00:16:31

### 48. Now that…,…

**意思：** 既然现在……，那么……（情况已变化）

> And now that I'm doing the job alone, I get the full 1,500. We agree upon that?

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:17:30–00:17:36

### 49. You got this.

**意思：** 你能搞定（鼓励）

> You got this, okay? No problem.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:17:48–00:17:50

### 50. At any point,…

**意思：** 在任何时候／只要某个时候……

> Yeah. At any point, it's too much, you're uncomfortable... we'll turn around and come home. You want maybe like a safe word?

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:18:05–00:18:12

### 51. That'll work.

**意思：** 那样可以／可行

> That'll work. Okay.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:18:15–00:18:17

### 52. leave sth. on

**意思：** 让某物保持开启（是否忘关由语境判断）

> I must have left my stupid phone on last night. It's dead.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:18:58–00:19:01

### 53. I couldn't have… if I wanted to.

**意思：** 就算想……也办不到（过去情境）

> Oh, I couldn't have stopped them if I wanted to.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:20:54–00:20:57

### 54. to date

**意思：** 到目前为止（较正式）

> To date, we have 68 very willing plaintiffs... signed to the class-action with hopes of raising that number to 350 or more.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:21:13–00:21:22

### 55. be a slam dunk

**意思：** 是十拿九稳的事（篮球比喻）

> Well, no case is ever a slam dunk.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:21:35–00:21:38

### 56. feel confident about sth.

**意思：** 对……有信心

> But I feel as confident about this as anything that's come through our doors.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:21:38–00:21:42

### 57. Can we slow down a second and…?

**意思：** 能不能先缓一下，再……？（暂停推进、提出讨论）

> But can we slow down a second and discuss terms, maybe?

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:21:58–00:22:01

### 58. down the road

**意思：** 往后／未来某个时候

> That's a few years down the road. As for money for you now...

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:22:20–00:22:24

### 59. As for sth.,…

**意思：** 至于……，……（转到另一话题）

> That's a few years down the road. As for money for you now... we don't do referral fees. Chuck will agree.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:22:20–00:22:27

### 60. What we could do is do sth.

**意思：** 我们可以采取的办法是……

> But what we could do is arrange an of-counsel fee... for the time you've already put in. Time that you spent building the case.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:22:30–00:22:37

### 61. put in time

**意思：** 投入／花费时间

> But what we could do is arrange an of-counsel fee... for the time you've already put in. Time that you spent building the case.

**来源：** S01E09 · 00:22:30–00:22:37

### 62. go as high as + 数字

**意思：** 到底什么如此……，让你非得……？（追问动机）

**意思：** 唯一说得通的解释是……

**意思：** 将一生投入……

**意思：** 收获回报／享受成果

**意思：** 在某种程度／内心某一层面

## S01E10

### 1. put money on sth.

### 6. set sb. up

**意思：** 给某人来一杯酒（酒吧语境；不同于设局／安排）

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:01:13–00:01:14

### 7. That'll be the day.

**意思：** 真会有那一天才怪（表示不信会发生）

**意思：** 赶时间

> Marco, settle down, okay? I'm in a rush.

### 9. skip bail

**意思：** 保释期间潜逃

### 10. save sb.'s ass

**意思：** 救某人一把／让某人免遭麻烦（粗俗口语）

> Chuck flew in and saved my ass.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:01:59–00:02:02

### 11. It's time to make some changes.

**意思：** 是时候作出一些改变了（决定换个活法／做法）

### 12. The same goes for sb./sth.

**意思：** ……也一样／同样适用

### 27. I never wanted it to go this way.

### 134. I can't imagine what you did to make this happen.

**意思：** 难以想象你为了促成这件事做了多少（表达感激）

> Kim, I can't imagine what you did to make this happen. Thank you.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:42:55–00:43:01

### 135. make sth. as clear as possible

**意思：** 把……尽可能说明白

> That's to make it as clear as possible.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:43:38–00:43:40

### 136. on the… front

**意思：** 在……方面／关于……这块（引入话题）

> Well, on the grocery front, almost everything was right this time. - Great. - Almost.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:43:42–00:43:47

### 137. It's a small thing, but…

**意思：** 这只是件小事，不过……（缓和意见）

> Um, it's a small thing, but to my taste, the, uh, Granny Smith apples are a bit too tart.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:43:47–00:43:52

### 138. to my taste

**意思：** 按我的口味／就我的喜好来说

> Um, it's a small thing, but to my taste, the, uh, Granny Smith apples are a bit too tart.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:43:47–00:43:52

### 139. be in season

**意思：** 正当季

> Fujis should be in season.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:43:58–00:44:00

### 140. attention to detail

**意思：** 对细节的留意／重视

> I appreciate your attention to detail.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:44:12–00:44:16

### 141. just to be sure

**意思：** 只是为了确保万无一失

> You know, just to be sure.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:44:21–00:44:23

### 142. I'd say we could…, but…

**意思：** 我本想提议……，可是……（解释为何没法提议）

> I'd say we could grab a beer, but they're waiting on all that paperwork back at the office...

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:44:58–00:45:02

### 143. be waiting on sth.

**意思：** 等着……（此处等文书送回）

> but they're waiting on all that paperwork back at the office...

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:45:00–00:45:02

### 144. Don't I, though?

**意思：** 我可太知道了（附和前句，强烈肯定）

> Don't I, though?

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:45:03–00:45:05

### 145. do business with sb.

**意思：** 与某人做生意／有业务往来

> You done business with him before? Right. Yeah. I'll be there.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:45:47–00:45:56

### 146. Help me out here.

**意思：** 帮我弄明白一下／帮我确认一下（此处求解释）

> - No charge. - Help me out here. Did I dream it, or did I have $1,600,000 on my desk in cash?

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:47:05–00:47:14

### 147. Did I dream it, or did…?

**意思：** 是我记错／在做梦，还是确实……？（强调不可思议）

> Did I dream it, or did I have $1,600,000 on my desk in cash?

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:47:08–00:47:14

### 148. on God's green earth

**意思：** 在这世上（加强语气）

> No one on God's green earth knew we had it.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:47:20–00:47:23

### 149. We could have done sth.

**意思：** 我们本来可以……（过去未采取的选择）

> We could have split it 50/50. We could have gone home with $800,000 each, tax-free.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:47:23–00:47:31

### 150. Your point being?

**意思：** 你想表达的重点是？（要求说明意图）

> Your point being?

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:47:31–00:47:33

### 151. That's as far as it goes.

**意思：** 事情就到此为止／仅限于此（限定职责范围）

> Me, personally... I was hired to do a job. I did it. That's as far as it goes.

**来源：** S01E10 · 00:47:50–00:47:56

## S02E01

### 1. a group effort

**意思：** 大家一起完成的事；把功劳归于团队

> Well, it's a group effort.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:06:33–00:06:34

### 2. knock it out of the park

**意思：** 做得特别出色；口语赞扬

> I mean, the folks at HHM are just knockin' it out of the park.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:06:34–00:06:37

### 3. Here's the thing.

**意思：** 事情是这样的／关键在于……；引出重点

> Listen, here's the thing.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:07:05–00:07:07

### 4. One thing has nothing to do with the other.

**意思：** 这两件事没有关系；切断关联

> One thing has absolutely nothing to do with the other.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:07:46–00:07:51

### 5. take myself out of the running

**意思：** 退出候选／竞争；此处婉拒工作机会

> It's an honor to be considered, but at this time... At this time, um, I'm gonna have to take myself out of the running.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:08:08–00:08:16

### 6. You'll thank me later.

**意思：** 以后你会感谢我的；劝对方接受建议

> You'll thank me later.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:09:57–00:09:59

### 7. on your own time

**意思：** 在你自己的时间；此处指别占用工作时间

> Good. Then you be proud of it on your own time, but not with me.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:12:03–00:12:08

### 8. watch your back

**意思：** 保护你／留意你的身后；此处指有人照应

> do not go to that meet without someone watching your back.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:12:45–00:12:48

### 9. the gravy train

**意思：** 轻松获利的好差事／财路；口语比喻

> Well, I'm sorry, but this is the gravy train, and it's leaving the station right now.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:12:53–00:13:00

### 10. Your services are no longer needed.

**意思：** 不再需要你的服务；正式、冷淡的解聘说法

> Your services are no longer needed.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:13:16–00:13:20

### 11. see things differently

**意思：** 看法不同；委婉说明分歧

> Yeah, saw things differently. So... You know how it is.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:14:16–00:14:21

### 12. Be my guest.

**意思：** 请便／随意；允许对方做某事

> Oh, be my guest. Get in.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:14:40–00:14:42

### 13. off the line

**意思：** 刚下生产线／新出厂；此处指新车

> Brand-new, off the line.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:15:01–00:15:02

### 14. Do your thing.

**意思：** 按你的方式做／做你要做的事；口语

> Do your thing. Make sure it's right.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:15:27–00:15:30

### 15. You're welcome to do sth.

**意思：** 你可以随意做某事；此处礼貌允许对方清点

> And, as always, you're welcome to count 'em.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:15:52–00:15:55

### 16. walk out on sth.

**意思：** 放弃／退出某事；此处指放弃工作机会

> Clearly explain to me why you walked out on the best job opportunity of your life.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:17:17–00:17:21

### 17. nothing fancy

**意思：** 不用太讲究／普通的就行

> Get a one-piece, nothing fancy.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:17:32–00:17:33

### 18. Simple as that.

**意思：** 就这么简单；结束解释

> I quit it. Simple as that.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:18:15–00:18:16

### 19. charge sth. to the room

**意思：** 把费用记到房账上

> And we can charge that to the room, can't we?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:18:40–00:18:43

### 20. Dealer's choice.

**意思：** 你来选／交给你决定；借用牌局说法

> Whatever. Dealer's choice.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:18:56–00:18:58

### 21. Where did that come from?

**意思：** 你怎么突然说这个？；追问意外的话题

> Asking if we have a future. I mean, where did that come from?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:19:26–00:19:33

### 22. bend over backwards to do sth.

**意思：** 竭尽全力去做某事；常指过度迁就

> Ever since I got here, all I've done is try to make Chuck happy, bend over backwards to please Chuck.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:19:44–00:19:52

### 23. for all the wrong reasons

**意思：** 出于完全不对的动机

> I got into the law for all the wrong reasons.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:20:01–00:20:04

### 24. trust my instincts

**意思：** 相信自己的直觉

> I'm trusting my instincts.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:20:04–00:20:06

### 25. be better spent elsewhere

**意思：** 用在别处更有价值；此处指才华

> I think that my talents are better spent elsewhere.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:20:06–00:20:09

### 26. have its moments

**意思：** 也有过不错的时候；对整体评价有所保留

> I'm not saying it didn't have its moments, but the stuff I liked about it, selling people, convincing people, I don't have to be a lawyer to do that.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:20:18–00:20:26

### 27. It's got no legs.

**意思：** 它没有发展后劲／支撑不了多久；此处指股票

> That stock is useless. It's got no legs.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:20:47–00:20:49

### 28. roll in

**意思：** 源源而来／到账；此处指钱进账

> Float around and wait for your Sandpiper money to roll in?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:21:03–00:21:06

### 29. have no bearing on sth.

**意思：** 与某事毫无关系／不影响某事；偏正式

> Sandpiper has no bearing on this at all.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:21:08–00:21:12

### 30. walk away from sth.

**意思：** 放弃／离开某个机会

> It's a great opportunity, and you're walking away from it.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:21:30–00:21:34

### 31. Shouldn't you at least try… before…?

**意思：** 至少先试一试再……，不行吗？；委婉劝说

> Look, shouldn't you at least try the job before you say no?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:21:35–00:21:39

### 32. It's not for me.

**意思：** 这不适合我／我不想要；委婉拒绝

> Kim, I appreciate your concern, but it's not for me. I don't want it.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:21:41–00:21:46

### 33. toss sth. away

**意思：** 把某物白白丢掉；此处指多年努力

> All that effort, you're just gonna toss it away? That's the sunk cost fallacy.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:21:51–00:21:57

### 34. throw good money after bad

**意思：** 继续在已亏损的事上投入；越陷越深

> They throw good money after bad thinking they can turn their luck around.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:22:01–00:22:04

### 35. turn your luck around

**意思：** 扭转运气／局面

> They throw good money after bad thinking they can turn their luck around.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:22:01–00:22:04

### 36. Where has it gotten me?

**意思：** 这样做让我得到了什么？；反问付出是否值得

> I've been doing the right thing for all these years now, and where has it gotten me?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:22:16–00:22:20

### 37. Who needs that aggravation?

**意思：** 何必找那个麻烦？；反问

> Wall Street's for suckers. Who needs that aggravation?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:22:36–00:22:38

### 38. Follow my lead.

**意思：** 跟着我做；让对方照自己的示范行动

> Follow my lead.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:23:15–00:23:17

### 39. settle a bet

**意思：** 解决一个打赌的争议；此处用来搭话

> Hey, buddy, could you settle a bet for us?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:23:25–00:23:29

### 40. I don't want to make it sound like…

**意思：** 我不想把这事说得像……；预先缓和说法

> I don't want to make it sound like I was eavesdropping, but I kind of was.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:23:30–00:23:33

### 41. know your stuff

**意思：** 很懂行／对自己的领域很熟

> This guy sounds like he knows his stuff.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:23:47–00:23:50

### 42. put all your eggs in one basket

**意思：** 把所有希望／资源押在一处

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:24:37–00:24:41

### 43. in the neighborhood of + 数字

**意思：** 大约（某个数额）

> and he left us somewhere in the neighborhood of $1.4 million.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:24:56–00:25:03

### 44. Fortune favors the bold.

**意思：** 幸运眷顾敢于冒险的人；劝说套语

> Fortune favors the bold, man.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:25:14–00:25:16

### 45. the tried and true

**意思：** 久经考验的稳妥选择

> Shoot for the tried and true.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:25:30–00:25:31

### 46. That's where sb. comes in.

**意思：** 这正是某人发挥作用的时候；推介自己

> Well, that's where someone like myself comes in.

**意思：** 有经验证的过往业绩／可靠履历

### 48. take calculated risks

**意思：** 承担经过权衡的风险

> I take calculated but aggressive risks

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:25:50–00:25:52

### 49. no obligations

**意思：** 没有必须承担的后续义务；推销时减轻压力

> No obligations. We can just rap about this a little bit.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:26:05–00:26:10

### 50. I won't bite.

**意思：** 别怕我／尽管过来；戏谑地打消顾虑

> I won't bite.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:26:41–00:26:43

### 51. to be precise

**意思：** 确切地说；修正前述说法

> Well, Boer, to be precise.

### 52. could use sth.

**意思：** 用得上一些……／需要一些……；委婉请求

> so we could use a wine list, if you get a chance...

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:27:14–00:27:17

### 53. if you get a chance

**意思：** 你方便的时候；放在请求后缓和语气

> so we could use a wine list, if you get a chance...

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:27:14–00:27:17

### 54. be down for sth.

**意思：** 愿意参加／接受某事；随意口语

> Yes. You down for that? You ever try Zafiro Anejo? No, I have not, but I'm down for whatever.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:27:20–00:27:27

### 55. the right time to jump in

**意思：** 正是入场的好时机；此处指投资

> And you're picking the right time to jump in.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:27:34–00:27:35

### 56. compliments of sb.

**意思：** 由某人赠送／请客；服务场景

> Compliments of the bartender. She thought you might want a souvenir.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:27:51–00:27:54

### 57. We're ready for the check.

**意思：** 我们准备结账了；餐厅用语

> I think we're ready for the check.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:27:59–00:28:02

### 58. to be clear

**意思：** 说清楚一点／为确认起见

> But, so, to be clear, you take the money directly from our account and invest it.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:28:10–00:28:16

### 59. and the what-have-you

**意思：** 诸如此类等等；随意、含糊的补充

> And then all the dividends and all the profits and the what-have-you comes back into our account.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:28:17–00:28:24

### 60. get out of your hair

**意思：** 不再打扰你／先告辞；口语

> All right, well, we should get out of your hair.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:28:51–00:28:53

### 61. the fine print

**意思：** 合同或单据上的细则／小字条款

> - I didn't read the fine print. - Wait.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:29:52–00:29:54

### 62. I got this.

**意思：** 我能搞定／我来处理；此处指自己想办法

> I got this.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:31:13–00:31:15

### 63. I'm fine with this.

**意思：** 这样我可以接受／我没意见；此处在争执后说

> Good. I'm fine with this.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:31:19–00:31:20

### 64. That's a keeper.

**意思：** 这个值得留住／这个人值得珍惜；此处带玩笑意味

> That's a keeper.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:31:45–00:31:47

### 65. Wouldn't it be great if we could…?

**意思：** 要是我们能……该多好；设想愿望的句式

> Hey, wouldn't it be great if we could do that every night? Yes, it would. But we can't. I know, yeah. But I'm just saying, if we could, it would be great. But I know we can't.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:31:52–00:32:14

### 66. I'm just saying…

**意思：** 我只是说说／我的意思是……；缓和立场

> But I'm just saying, if we could, it would be great.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:32:07–00:32:11

### 67. dole out assignments

**意思：** 分派任务；dole out 常有逐一分发之感

> Howard is doling out assignments,

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:32:19–00:32:20

### 68. get stuck in sth.

**意思：** 被困在某项任务里；此处指文件审查。英式 get stuck in 也可指积极投入，须看语境

> Howard is doling out assignments, and the last one through the door could get stuck in doc review.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:32:19–00:32:25

### 69. common knowledge

**意思：** 众所周知的事

> Was it common knowledge that you have baseball cards?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:33:44–00:33:48

### 70. nobody in particular

**意思：** 没有特定的人；此处询问有无明确嫌疑人

> So, nobody in particular you can think of wanted 'em?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:33:49–00:33:52

### 71. a fair bit

**意思：** 不少／相当多；口语数量表达

> Cash, a fair bit, uh...

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:34:15–00:34:17

### 72. be exposed to the elements

**意思：** 暴露在外界环境中；此处指卡片离开保护套

> but if they're taken out and exposed to the elements, I mean, or creased, God forbid.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:34:36–00:34:42

### 73. in a matter of seconds

**意思：** 短短几秒之内

> I mean, you're looking at a nine-point downgrade in a matter of seconds.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:34:42–00:34:45

### 74. the more…, the less…

**意思：** 越……就越不……；此处指拖越久，找回概率越低（字幕后半句较别扭）

> So, the more time passes, I mean, the less likely we are of a recovery.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:34:50–00:34:55

### 75. toss a place

**意思：** 把一个地方翻得乱七八糟；此处指搜翻房间

> Well, they really tossed this place.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:35:05–00:35:06

### 76. Just wondering your thoughts.

**意思：** 只是想听听你的看法；试探性提问

> I don't know. Just wondering your thoughts.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:35:11–00:35:15

### 77. the crown jewels

**意思：** 最珍贵的部分；此处指收藏中最好的卡片

> But these were the crown jewels over here.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:35:21–00:35:25

### 78. make sense of sth.

**意思：** 弄明白某事／解释某事

> Just trying to make sense of it. What's to make sense of?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:35:33–00:35:36

### 79. go nuts

**意思：** 发疯似地行动／失控；此处指破坏

> Or hopped up on speed or whatever and went nuts with the vandalism?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:35:40–00:35:45

### 80. be hopped up on speed

**意思：** 服了兴奋剂而亢奋；粗俗口语，speed 指毒品

> Or hopped up on speed or whatever and went nuts with the vandalism?

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:35:40–00:35:45

### 81. get a great deal on sth.

**意思：** 买到很划算的东西／拿到好条件

> Got a great deal on it.

**来源：** S02E01 · 00:35:57–00:35:59

### 82. look through the wrong end of the telescope

**意思：** 看错了重点／把事情看反了；比喻

> But I think we're lookin' through the wrong end of the telescope here.

**意思：** 想出某个点子／说法

**意思：** 播下怀疑／制造疑点

**意思：** 最离谱／最突出；常为负面评价

**意思：** 最终使事情成功／盖棺定论

> ...I think it was the video that clinched it.

**意思：** 不得不说……；引出评价，口语

**意思：** 在细枝末节上较真；钻牛角尖

**意思：** 仍然毫不知情

**意思：** 玩火／冒危险

**来源：** S02E02 · 00:45:06–00:45:09

### 106. if I remember correctly

**意思：** 如果我没记错的话；为记忆留余地

### 107. screw around

**意思：** 瞎闹／胡来；粗俗口语

**意思：** 继续这样做；常暗示坏后果

## S02E03

### 1. Sorry about the hiccup.

**意思：** 迟早

**意思：** 大意如此／起这个作用；此处指备忘录说明上述结论

**意思：** 你方便的时候／有空再看；礼貌、略正式

## S02E04

### 1. all in

**意思：** 总计／全部算上；此处指广告总成本

**意思：** 从一开始

**意思：** 主动采取行动

**意思：** 学习适应过程；此处指融入团队

## S02E07

**来源：** S03E02 · 342–344，00:34:22,654–00:34:31,264

### 61. I'm a little hazy on …

**意思：** 我对……不太清楚／记不清

**来源：** S03E02 · 357–358，00:35:00,294–00:35:06,674

### 62. have a buffer

**意思：** 有一层隔离／缓冲；本句指通过中间人减少法律风险的设想，并非可靠法律建议

> but if I tell you what I heard and not Jimmy directly... like... like we have a buffer... - ... then legally, I'm safe? - Ernie.

**来源：** S03E02 · 359–362，00:35:06,675–00:35:17,268

### 63. the rest of the way

**意思：** 余下的过程／接下来的部分

> - I'll tell them. - Now, you remember Francesca. She'll take care of you the rest of the way.

**来源：** S03E02 · 373–374，00:35:46,006–00:35:50,436

### 64. set the stage for sb.

**意思：** 向某人交代背景，铺垫接下来要讲的事；此处不是安排舞台

> Kim, let me set the stage for you, okay? When I went to Chuck's place last week... it was like the inside of a Jiffy Pop wrapper, all right?

**来源：** S03E02 · 401–403，00:36:46,547–00:36:53,633

### 65. a broken man

**意思：** 精神受到重创、已垮掉的人；人物状态搭配

**来源：** S03E02 · 410–411，00:37:07,151–00:37:11,571

### 66. Sorry for the delay.

**意思：** 抱歉让你久等／耽搁了；恢复接待时的套语

**来源：** S03E02 · 442–443，00:39:00,097–00:39:03,933

### 67. I would never have thought to do sth.

**意思：** 我怎么也不会想到要做某事；强调想法出人意料

**来源：** S03E02 · 445–446，00:39:07,715–00:39:12,052

### 68. sort sth. by date

**意思：** 将某物按日期排序

**来源：** S03E02 · 467–470，00:40:24,676–00:40:31,949

### 69. get off the phone with sb.

**意思：** 结束与某人的电话

> just got off the phone with my old Crim-Pro professor.

**来源：** S03E02 · 474，00:40:41,442–00:40:44,058

### 70. poke holes in sth.

**意思：** 挑出某种说法／证据的漏洞

**来源：** S03E02 · 482–483，00:41:02,547–00:41:07,551

### 71. failing that

**意思：** 如果那样还不行／如果前面的方法失败

> We can poke holes in the custody... throw doubt the voice on the tape is even yours. Even failing that, its probative value doesn't outweigh how prejudicial it is.

**来源：** S03E02 · 482–485，00:41:02,547–00:41:11,513

### 72. probative value

**意思：** 证据证明力；法律术语搭配

> Even failing that, its probative value doesn't outweigh how prejudicial it is.

**来源：** S03E02 · 484–485，00:41:07,552–00:41:11,513

### 73. get sth. excluded outright

**意思：** 使某物直接被排除；此处指排除证据

> I think we can get the whole thing bounced under 403. Probably get it excluded outright.

**来源：** S03E02 · 486–487，00:41:11,514–00:41:17,105

### 74. act accordingly

**意思：** 据此行动／按情况采取相应措施

> If Chuck has a plan for this tape, I don't know what it is. At this point, all we can do is wait for his move, then act accordingly.

**来源：** S03E02 · 495–497，00:41:45,506–00:41:53,212

### 75. the cost is starting to add up

**意思：** 费用开始不断累积／越来越可观

> I think I've been very patient... but the cost of these around-the-clock private investigators, is really starting to add up.

**来源：** S03E02 · 526–528，00:48:03,104–00:48:12,032

### 76. have an ethical obligation to do sth.

**意思：** 有职业伦理义务做某事；本句在to之后被打断，不能补造角色后面实际说了什么

> Howard. This is our duty. As officers of the court... - we have an ethical obligation to... - Yes.

**来源：** S03E02 · 529–531，00:48:12,057–00:48:19,369

### 77. sit back and do nothing

**意思：** 袖手旁观／坐等而不采取行动

### 78. be open to alternate strategies

**意思：** 愿意考虑其他策略，而不只坚持当前办法

> I'm not talking about doing nothing. I just want to... free up our minds... and be open to alternate strategies, please.

**来源：** S03E02 · 534–537，00:48:23,336–00:48:32,685

### 79. I wouldn't put anything past sb.

**意思：** 某人什么都干得出来；带怀疑或负面评价

> Jimmy will most likely break in while he thinks I'm sleeping. I mean, I wouldn't put anything past him.

**来源：** S03E02 · 542–543，00:48:47,064–00:48:52,748

### 80. under cover of darkness

**意思：** 趁夜色掩护／利用黑暗隐蔽行动

> It just makes the most sense... he'll try to steal the tape under cover of darkness.

**来源：** S03E02 · 544–545，00:48:52,773–00:48:56,531

### 81. burn sth. to the ground

**意思：** 把某物彻底烧毁

> Or did you make copies? Huh, Chuck? Huh? You tell me, or I'll burn this whole goddamn house to the ground.

**来源：** S03E02 · 578–579，00:50:23,422–00:50:29,391

### 82. be a witness to sth.

**意思：** 亲眼见证某事／是某事的目击者

**来源：** S03E02 · 583–587，00:50:37,366–00:50:46,212

## S03E03

### 1. May I assume (that) …?

**意思：** 我可以认为……吗？礼貌而明确地核对假设

> May I assume that you are armed?

**来源：** S03E03 · 16，00:02:50,683–00:02:54,085

### 2. Are we in agreement?

**意思：** 我们达成一致了吗？正式核对约定

> Are we in agreement? We are.

**来源：** S03E03 · 21–22，00:03:02,295–00:03:06,497

### 3. Care to elaborate?

**意思：** 能具体说说吗？请对方解释含糊说法

> You care to elaborate?

**来源：** S03E03 · 24，00:04:14,840–00:04:17,974

### 4. be in sb.'s interest

**意思：** 符合某人的利益／对某人有利

> It's not in my interest for Hector Salamanca to die... at this time.

**来源：** S03E03 · 25–26，00:04:18,947–00:04:25,952

### 5. How very …

**意思：** 可多么……啊；以貌似赞扬的语气反讽，句中是嫌解释含糊

> Who is he to you? An associate of an associate. Mm. How very specific.

**来源：** S03E03 · 27–30，00:04:25,987–00:04:34,094

### 6. settle the matter

**意思：** 把事情解决／了结

> And yet, still, you robbed his truck. Shouldn't that have settled the matter?

**来源：** S03E03 · 38–39，00:04:51,646–00:04:57,250

### 7. make an attempt on sb.'s life

**意思：** 企图杀害某人／行刺某人

> Most men would have walked away, but instead... you made an attempt on his life.

**来源：** S03E03 · 40–42，00:04:57,285–00:05:06,559

### 8. not be completely unsympathetic to sth.

**意思：** 并非完全不能理解／同情某事；用双重否定表达有限认同

> However, I am not completely unsympathetic to your... sense of justice.

**来源：** S03E03 · 49–50，00:05:39,327–00:05:44,864

### 9. a sense of justice

**意思：** 正义感／对公平的诉求

> However, I am not completely unsympathetic to your... sense of justice.

**来源：** S03E03 · 49–50，00:05:39,327–00:05:44,864

### 10. in the same manner

**意思：** 以同样的方式

> And if you were to hurt him in the same manner again, I would not stand in your way.

**来源：** S03E03 · 55–56，00:05:54,176–00:06:02,449

### 11. if you feel so inclined

**意思：** 如果你愿意这么做；较正式地把选择交给对方

> You want me to rob another truck. If you feel so inclined.

**来源：** S03E03 · 57–58，00:06:02,484–00:06:08,221

### 12. Our business here is finished.

**意思：** 我们在这里要谈／办的事结束了；正式收束

> I'm done with that. Then our business here is finished.

**来源：** S03E03 · 59–60，00:06:08,256–00:06:15,228

### 13. Just like that?

**意思：** 就这样？／这么简单？可质疑过程过于轻易

> Just like that? Just like that.

**来源：** S03E03 · 65–66，00:06:22,070–00:06:25,672

**意思：** 打断供应渠道／破坏补给线

> You want his trucks hit because you want to disrupt his supply line.

**来源：** S03E03 · 71–72，00:06:52,033–00:06:58,838

### 15. Something's come up.

**意思：** 临时有事／出现了意外情况；解释改约

> Listen, um, something's come up, and, uh, I need you to reschedule all my appointments for tomorrow.

**意思：** 重新安排预约时间

### 17. This is for the best.

**意思：** 这样做终究最好／是最好的结果；安慰或合理化选择

**意思：** 面对后果／承担后果

### 19. come out the other side a better person

### 20. make a change before it's too late

**意思：** 趁还来得及作出改变

**意思：** 找到自己的道路／人生方向；比喻搭配

### 22. curl up in sth.

**意思：** 蜷缩在某物里；此处指裹在毯子中

**意思：** 将某人连接到机器／医疗设备上

**意思：** 接我的车来了；ride指提供接送的车辆／人

**意思：** 曾经风光的人如今落到这般田地；常含讽刺

### 26. throw the book at sb.

**意思：** 对某人从严惩罚／尽可能严厉地起诉

### 27. for old times' sake

**意思：** 看在过去的情分上／为了怀旧

**来源：** S03E03 · 159，00:12:27,635–00:12:30,254

### 28. above sb.'s pay grade

**意思：** 超出某人的职责／权限范围；不只是工资高低

**意思：** 比……好多了／强过……；原句省略It

## S03E04

### 1. get used to sth.

There's no way you were gonna let him destroy the only copy of that tape, so you made a duplicate... - it's the first thing you did. - Kim...

## S03E05

### 1. We're almost there.

### 3. the right play

**意思：** 正确的应对策略／这一招是否合适

**意思：** ……是怎么回事？询问不寻常的情况

**意思：** 给某人打电话联系

**意思：** 凑合着过／在简陋条件下生活

### 8. Then it's settled.

### 59. evidentiary value

### 60. another side to the story

**意思：** 事情还有另一面／另一种解释

One not about calculation... or ill intent, but about about two brothers, whose relationship, after years of strain, finally broke.

### 62. have the complete picture

**意思：** 掌握完整情况／看到事情全貌

### 63. in good standing

**意思：** 资格有效、信誉良好、未受纪律处分；机构成员身份

### 68. have a lot of get-up-and-go

**意思：** 很有干劲／行动力强

**意思：** 靠自身努力和有限资源取得某种机会或资格

### 71. damage morale

### 72. give sb. some leeway

### 83. Keep me posted.

### 94. take time to do sth.

### 106. out of sight

**意思：** 藏在看不见的地方

**意思：** 描述事情发生时的背景和环境

Well, the circumstances of the recording are materially relevant. How can you know what the tape really means unless you know what was going on when it was recorded?

### 108. go all out

**意思：** 不遗余力／把能做的都做足

**意思：** 夸大／突出某种情况；此处夸张病情

**意思：** 使某种讨论或行动有了可能；此处指引入议题

**意思：** 胸口发紧／胸闷

**意思：** 逐渐减弱／减少；本句指强度随距离增大而下降，与送人／物到某地的drop off义不同

## S03E06

### 1. You're light.

**意思：** 你交的钱／货数量不够；light此处指不足

**来源：** S03E06 · 31–36，00:03:09,281–00:03:35,041

### 2. make it up on sb.'s end

**意思：** 从自己这边补足差额

**来源：** S03E06 · 32–39，00:03:20,717–00:03:45,891

### 3. build a practice

**意思：** 建立个人执业业务／开拓客户群；这里指律师事务

**来源：** S03E06 · 63–66，00:06:42,336–00:06:53,031
